class_name AnalogScopeEngine
extends RefCounted

enum CouplingMode {
	DC,
	AC,
	GND
}

enum TriggerSlope {
	RISING,
	FALLING
}

enum ProbeTarget {
	FUNC_GEN,
	CAL_1VPP,
	FLOATING
}

const COUPLING_NAMES := ["DC", "AC", "GND"]
const SLOPE_NAMES := ["Rising (+)", "Falling (-)"]
const PROBE_TARGET_NAMES := ["Function Generator OUT", "Scope CAL (1 Vp-p 1kHz)", "Floating (Disconnected)"]

const AC_COUPLING_FC := 10.0
const AMP_RAIL_VOLTS := 40.0
const DELAY_LINE_SEC := 0.00005

static func process_chain(
	raw_times: PackedFloat64Array,
	raw_voltages: PackedFloat64Array,
	dt: float,
	analog_cfg: Dictionary,
	view_cfg: Dictionary
) -> Dictionary:
	var n := raw_voltages.size()
	var coupled := PackedFloat64Array()
	var attenuated := PackedFloat64Array()
	var preamp := PackedFloat64Array()
	var delayed := PackedFloat64Array()
	var vert_deflection := PackedFloat64Array()
	var trigger_pulse := PackedFloat64Array()
	var sweep_ramp := PackedFloat64Array()

	coupled.resize(n)
	attenuated.resize(n)
	preamp.resize(n)
	delayed.resize(n)
	vert_deflection.resize(n)
	trigger_pulse.resize(n)
	sweep_ramp.resize(n)

	if n == 0:
		return {
			"nodes": {},
			"sweep_times": PackedFloat64Array(),
			"sweep_volts": PackedFloat64Array(),
			"triggered": false,
			"trigger_index": 0
		}

	var coupling: int = analog_cfg.get("coupling", CouplingMode.DC)
	var probe_atten: float = analog_cfg.get("probe_attenuation", 1.0)
	var probe_comp: float = analog_cfg.get("probe_compensation", 1.0) # 0.65 (under) .. 1.0 (flat) .. 1.35 (over)
	var vert_pos_div: float = analog_cfg.get("vert_pos_div", 0.0)
	var volts_per_div: float = maxf(float(view_cfg.get("volts_per_div", 1.0)), 1e-6)
	var time_per_div: float = maxf(float(view_cfg.get("time_per_div", 0.0005)), 1e-9)
	var safe_dt := maxf(dt, 1e-12)

	# 1. Input Coupling Stage (N2_COUPLING)
	var rc := 1.0 / (TAU * AC_COUPLING_FC)
	var alpha := rc / (rc + safe_dt)
	for i in range(n):
		var vin := raw_voltages[i]
		match coupling:
			CouplingMode.GND:
				coupled[i] = 0.0
			CouplingMode.DC:
				coupled[i] = vin
			CouplingMode.AC:
				if i == 0:
					coupled[i] = 0.0
				else:
					coupled[i] = alpha * (coupled[i - 1] + (vin - raw_voltages[i - 1]))

	# 2. Attenuator (N3_ATTENUATOR) + Probe Compensation RC Edge Effect & Preamp Gain (N4_PREAMP)
	# Preamp scales attenuated signal by vertical sensitivity gain Av = probe_atten / volts_per_div
	var div_factor := 1.0 / maxf(probe_atten, 1.0)
	var preamp_gain := probe_atten / volts_per_div
	var comp_state := 0.0
	var comp_decay := exp(-safe_dt / 0.00008)

	for i in range(n):
		var v_att := coupled[i] * div_factor
		if probe_atten > 1.5 and i > 0:
			var dv := v_att - (coupled[i - 1] * div_factor)
			comp_state = comp_state * comp_decay + dv * (probe_comp - 1.0) * 1.4
			v_att += comp_state
		attenuated[i] = v_att
		preamp[i] = clampf(v_att * preamp_gain, -AMP_RAIL_VOLTS, AMP_RAIL_VOLTS)

	# 3. Delay Line (N5_DELAY_LINE) & Vertical Deflection Plates (N6_VERT_DEFLECT)
	var delay_samples := clampi(int(round(DELAY_LINE_SEC / safe_dt)), 1, maxi(n / 8, 1))
	for i in range(n):
		var src_i := maxi(i - delay_samples, 0)
		delayed[i] = preamp[src_i]
		# Convert division-scaled preamp output back to screen volts + vertical position offset
		vert_deflection[i] = (delayed[i] + vert_pos_div) * volts_per_div

	# 4. Trigger Comparator (N7_TRIGGER)
	var trig_level: float = analog_cfg.get("trigger_level", 0.0)
	var trig_slope: int = analog_cfg.get("trigger_slope", TriggerSlope.RISING)
	var hysteresis: float = analog_cfg.get("trigger_hysteresis", 0.05)

	var trig_idx := -1
	var armed := false
	var sweep_samples := maxi(int(round((time_per_div * 10.0) / safe_dt)) + 1, 2)
	var max_search := maxi(n - sweep_samples, 1)

	for i in range(1, max_search):
		var v_prev := coupled[i - 1]
		var v_curr := coupled[i]
		if trig_slope == TriggerSlope.RISING:
			if v_prev <= trig_level - hysteresis:
				armed = true
			if armed and v_prev < trig_level and v_curr >= trig_level:
				trig_idx = i
				break
		else:
			if v_prev >= trig_level + hysteresis:
				armed = true
			if armed and v_prev > trig_level and v_curr <= trig_level:
				trig_idx = i
				break

	var triggered := trig_idx >= 0
	var start_idx := trig_idx if triggered else 0

	var horiz_pos_div: float = analog_cfg.get("horiz_pos_div", 0.0)
	var shift_samples := int(round((horiz_pos_div * time_per_div) / safe_dt))
	start_idx = clampi(start_idx - shift_samples, 0, maxi(n - sweep_samples, 0))

	for i in range(n):
		var comp_high := (coupled[i] >= trig_level) if trig_slope == TriggerSlope.RISING else (coupled[i] <= trig_level)
		trigger_pulse[i] = 5.0 if comp_high else 0.0

		if i < start_idx:
			sweep_ramp[i] = -5.0
		else:
			var prog := clampf(float(i - start_idx) / float(maxi(sweep_samples - 1, 1)), 0.0, 1.0)
			sweep_ramp[i] = lerpf(-5.0, 5.0, prog)

	# 5. Extract 10-Div CRT Display Sweep
	var out_len := mini(sweep_samples, n - start_idx)
	var sweep_times := PackedFloat64Array()
	var sweep_volts := PackedFloat64Array()
	sweep_times.resize(out_len)
	sweep_volts.resize(out_len)

	var t0 := raw_times[start_idx]
	for k in range(out_len):
		sweep_times[k] = raw_times[start_idx + k] - t0
		sweep_volts[k] = vert_deflection[start_idx + k]

	var nodes := {
		"N1_INPUT": raw_voltages,
		"N2_COUPLING": coupled,
		"N3_ATTENUATOR": attenuated,
		"N4_PREAMP": preamp,
		"N5_DELAY_LINE": delayed,
		"N6_VERT_DEFLECT": vert_deflection,
		"N7_TRIGGER": trigger_pulse,
		"N8_SWEEP": sweep_ramp
	}

	return {
		"nodes": nodes,
		"sweep_times": sweep_times,
		"sweep_volts": sweep_volts,
		"triggered": triggered,
		"trigger_index": start_idx
	}

class_name AnalogCircuitExplorer
extends HBoxContainer

const STAGES := [
	{
		"id": "N1_INPUT",
		"title": "1. INPUT BNC",
		"sub": "1MΩ // 25pF",
		"branch": "VERT",
		"pos": Vector2(0.08, 0.25),
		"equation": "v_in(t) = V_off + A·sin(2πft + φ) + n(t)",
		"desc": "Coaxial BNC input terminal with overvoltage clamp diodes and standard 1 MΩ || 25 pF input impedance."
	},
	{
		"id": "N2_COUPLING",
		"title": "2. COUPLING",
		"sub": "AC / DC / GND",
		"branch": "VERT",
		"pos": Vector2(0.24, 0.25),
		"equation": "DC: v_out = v_in  |  GND: 0V  |  AC: H(s) = sRC/(1+sRC), fc = 10 Hz",
		"desc": "Selects direct DC path, 0V ground reference, or series DC-blocking high-pass capacitor."
	},
	{
		"id": "N3_ATTENUATOR",
		"title": "3. ATTENUATOR",
		"sub": "1x / 10x Div",
		"branch": "VERT",
		"pos": Vector2(0.40, 0.25),
		"equation": "v_att(t) = v_coup(t) · R2 / (R1 + R2)",
		"desc": "Frequency-compensated resistive-capacitive divider protecting the vertical preamp from high voltages."
	},
	{
		"id": "N4_PREAMP",
		"title": "4. VERT PREAMP",
		"sub": "Av · Vin (±40V)",
		"branch": "VERT",
		"pos": Vector2(0.56, 0.25),
		"equation": "v_amp(t) = clamp(Av · v_att(t), -40V, +40V)",
		"desc": "Low-noise JFET differential preamplifier scaling the attenuated signal with supply rail saturation."
	},
	{
		"id": "N5_DELAY_LINE",
		"title": "5. DELAY LINE",
		"sub": "τ_delay = 50 µs",
		"branch": "VERT",
		"pos": Vector2(0.72, 0.25),
		"equation": "v_del(t) = v_amp(t - τ_d)",
		"desc": "Delays the vertical waveform so the trigger & sweep generator can start the beam before the triggering edge arrives."
	},
	{
		"id": "N6_VERT_DEFLECT",
		"title": "6. VERT DEFLECT",
		"sub": "Y-Plates + Pos",
		"branch": "VERT",
		"pos": Vector2(0.89, 0.25),
		"equation": "y(t) = K_y · (v_del(t) + V_pos)",
		"desc": "Push-pull high-voltage deflection amplifier driving the CRT vertical electrostatic plates."
	},
	{
		"id": "N7_TRIGGER",
		"title": "7. TRIGGER",
		"sub": "Schmitt Comp",
		"branch": "TRIG",
		"pos": Vector2(0.36, 0.68),
		"equation": "Rising: v[n-1] < V_T and v[n] ≥ V_T (with hysteresis)",
		"desc": "Taps post-coupling vertical signal and fires a synchronized gate pulse when crossing Trigger Level."
	},
	{
		"id": "N8_SWEEP",
		"title": "8. SWEEP & X-AMP",
		"sub": "Miller Ramp",
		"branch": "TRIG",
		"pos": Vector2(0.66, 0.68),
		"equation": "x(t) = x_0 + S_x · (t - t_trig)   [-5V → +5V]",
		"desc": "Constant-current Miller integrator generating a linear horizontal sawtooth ramp across the CRT X-plates."
	}
]

const WIRES := [
	["N1_INPUT", "N2_COUPLING", "VERT"],
	["N2_COUPLING", "N3_ATTENUATOR", "VERT"],
	["N3_ATTENUATOR", "N4_PREAMP", "VERT"],
	["N4_PREAMP", "N5_DELAY_LINE", "VERT"],
	["N5_DELAY_LINE", "N6_VERT_DEFLECT", "VERT"],
	["N2_COUPLING", "N7_TRIGGER", "TRIG"],
	["N7_TRIGGER", "N8_SWEEP", "TRIG"]
]

var _diagram_canvas: Control
var _probe_canvas: Control
var _title_lbl: Label
var _eq_lbl: Label
var _desc_lbl: Label
var _params_lbl: Label
var _metrics_lbl: Label
var _flow_phase := 0.0

func _ready() -> void:
	add_theme_constant_override("separation", 12)
	_build_layout()
	if has_node("/root/SimState"):
		SimState.state_updated.connect(_refresh_inspector)
		SimState.selected_node_changed.connect(func(_id: String) -> void: _refresh_inspector())
	_refresh_inspector()

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	if has_node("/root/SimState") and SimState.view_config.get("power_on", true) and SimState.view_config.get("running", true):
		_flow_phase = fmod(_flow_phase + delta * 1.8, 1.0)
		if _diagram_canvas != null:
			_diagram_canvas.queue_redraw()

func _build_layout() -> void:
	# Left: Interactive Block Diagram
	var left_panel := PanelContainer.new()
	left_panel.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	left_panel.size_flags_stretch_ratio = 1.65
	var sb_left := StyleBoxFlat.new()
	sb_left.bg_color = Color(0.05, 0.07, 0.09, 1.0)
	sb_left.border_color = Color(0.20, 0.28, 0.34, 1.0)
	sb_left.set_border_width_all(2)
	sb_left.set_corner_radius_all(6)
	left_panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", sb_left)
	add_child(left_panel)

	_diagram_canvas = Control.new()
	_diagram_canvas.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	_diagram_canvas.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	_diagram_canvas.mouse_default_cursor_shape = Control.CURSOR_POINTING_HAND
	_diagram_canvas.draw.connect(_draw_block_diagram)
	_diagram_canvas.gui_input.connect(_on_diagram_input)
	left_panel.add_child(_diagram_canvas)

	# Right: Node Probe & Equation Inspector
	var right_panel := PanelContainer.new()
	right_panel.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	right_panel.size_flags_stretch_ratio = 1.0
	var sb_right := StyleBoxFlat.new()
	sb_right.bg_color = Color(0.07, 0.09, 0.12, 1.0)
	sb_right.border_color = Color(0.22, 0.32, 0.38, 1.0)
	sb_right.set_border_width_all(2)
	sb_right.set_corner_radius_all(6)
	right_panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", sb_right)
	add_child(right_panel)

	var pad := MarginContainer.new()
	pad.add_theme_constant_override("margin_left", 14)
	pad.add_theme_constant_override("margin_right", 14)
	pad.add_theme_constant_override("margin_top", 12)
	pad.add_theme_constant_override("margin_bottom", 12)
	right_panel.add_child(pad)

	var vbox := VBoxContainer.new()
	vbox.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 8)
	pad.add_child(vbox)

	var hdr := Label.new()
	hdr.text = "INTERNAL NODE PROBE INSPECTOR"
	hdr.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 13)
	hdr.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color(0.45, 0.85, 1.0, 1.0))
	vbox.add_child(hdr)

	_title_lbl = Label.new()
	_title_lbl.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 17)
	_title_lbl.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color(0.30, 1.0, 0.72, 1.0))
	vbox.add_child(_title_lbl)

	_eq_lbl = Label.new()
	_eq_lbl.autowrap_mode = TextServer.AUTOWRAP_WORD_SMART
	_eq_lbl.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 12)
	_eq_lbl.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color(1.0, 0.82, 0.35, 1.0))
	vbox.add_child(_eq_lbl)

	_desc_lbl = Label.new()
	_desc_lbl.autowrap_mode = TextServer.AUTOWRAP_WORD_SMART
	_desc_lbl.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 12)
	_desc_lbl.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color(0.76, 0.82, 0.88, 1.0))
	vbox.add_child(_desc_lbl)

	vbox.add_child(HSeparator.new())

	_params_lbl = Label.new()
	_params_lbl.autowrap_mode = TextServer.AUTOWRAP_WORD_SMART
	_params_lbl.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 12)
	_params_lbl.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color(0.85, 0.90, 0.95, 1.0))
	vbox.add_child(_params_lbl)

	_metrics_lbl = Label.new()
	_metrics_lbl.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 13)
	_metrics_lbl.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color(0.35, 0.98, 0.75, 1.0))
	vbox.add_child(_metrics_lbl)

	# Live Node Probe Mini-Oscilloscope
	_probe_canvas = Control.new()
	_probe_canvas.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(240, 180)
	_probe_canvas.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	_probe_canvas.draw.connect(_draw_node_probe_waveform)
	vbox.add_child(_probe_canvas)

func _get_stage_rect(stage: Dictionary, canvas_size: Vector2) -> Rect2:
	var box_w := clampf(canvas_size.x * 0.135, 92.0, 145.0)
	var box_h := 66.0
	var center := Vector2(stage["pos"].x * canvas_size.x, stage["pos"].y * canvas_size.y)
	return Rect2(center - Vector2(box_w * 0.5, box_h * 0.5), Vector2(box_w, box_h))

func _on_diagram_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if event is InputEventMouseButton:
		var mb := event as InputEventMouseButton
		if mb.pressed and mb.button_index == MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT:
			var sz := _diagram_canvas.size
			for stage in STAGES:
				if _get_stage_rect(stage, sz).has_point(mb.position):
					if has_node("/root/SimState"):
						SimState.select_circuit_node(stage["id"])
					break

func _draw_block_diagram() -> void:
	var sz := _diagram_canvas.size
	if sz.x < 40.0 or sz.y < 40.0:
		return
	var font := ThemeDB.fallback_font


  # Section Titles
	_diagram_canvas.draw_string(font, Vector2(18, 26), "NATIONAL VP-5231A — INTERNAL ANALOG SIGNAL & TIMEBASE ARCHITECTURE (Click Any Block to Probe)", HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 13, Color(0.75, 0.86, 0.92, 1.0))
	_diagram_canvas.draw_string(font, Vector2(18, sz.y * 0.10), "VERTICAL SIGNAL PATH (Y-DEFLECTION)", HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 11, Color(0.30, 0.95, 0.72, 0.85))
	_diagram_canvas.draw_string(font, Vector2(18, sz.y * 0.53), "TRIGGER & HORIZONTAL TIMEBASE PATH (X-DEFLECTION)", HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 11, Color(1.0, 0.76, 0.28, 0.85))

	var stage_rects := {}
	for stage in STAGES:
		stage_rects[stage["id"]] = _get_stage_rect(stage, sz)

	var power_on := true
	var selected_id := "N3_ATTENUATOR"
	if has_node("/root/SimState"):
		power_on = bool(SimState.view_config.get("power_on", true))
		selected_id = SimState.selected_circuit_node

	# Draw Connecting Wires & Animated Signal Pulses
	for wire in WIRES:
		var r_from: Rect2 = stage_rects[wire[0]]
		var r_to: Rect2 = stage_rects[wire[1]]
		var branch: String = wire[2]
		var p1 := Vector2(r_from.end.x, r_from.get_center().y)
		var p2 := Vector2(r_to.position.x, r_to.get_center().y)
		if wire[0] == "N2_COUPLING" and wire[1] == "N7_TRIGGER":
			p1 = Vector2(r_from.get_center().x, r_from.end.y)
			p2 = Vector2(r_to.position.x, r_to.get_center().y)

		var base_col := Color(0.22, 0.75, 0.58, 0.75) if branch == "VERT" else Color(0.88, 0.65, 0.22, 0.75)
		if not power_on:
			base_col = Color(0.25, 0.28, 0.30, 0.45)

		if wire[0] == "N2_COUPLING" and wire[1] == "N7_TRIGGER":
			var elbow := Vector2(p1.x, p2.y)
			_diagram_canvas.draw_line(p1, elbow, base_col, 2.0)
			_diagram_canvas.draw_line(elbow, p2, base_col, 2.0)
			if power_on:
				var pulse_pos := p1.lerp(elbow, _flow_phase * 2.0) if _flow_phase < 0.5 else elbow.lerp(p2, (_flow_phase - 0.5) * 2.0)
				_diagram_canvas.draw_circle(pulse_pos, 4.0, Color(1.0, 0.85, 0.35, 1.0))
		else:
			_diagram_canvas.draw_line(p1, p2, base_col, 2.2)
			# Arrowhead
			_diagram_canvas.draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([
				p2,
				p2 + Vector2(-7, -4),
				p2 + Vector2(-7, 4)
			]), base_col)
			if power_on:
				var pulse_pos := p1.lerp(p2, _flow_phase)
				var dot_col := Color(0.45, 1.0, 0.82, 1.0) if branch == "VERT" else Color(1.0, 0.85, 0.35, 1.0)
				_diagram_canvas.draw_circle(pulse_pos, 4.0, dot_col)

	# Draw Stage Blocks with Live Vpp Readout
	for stage in STAGES:
		var id: String = stage["id"]
		var r: Rect2 = stage_rects[id]
		var is_sel := (id == selected_id)
		var is_vert: bool = (stage["branch"] == "VERT")

		var fill_col := Color(0.11, 0.16, 0.20, 1.0) if not is_sel else Color(0.14, 0.26, 0.30, 1.0)
		var border_col := (Color(0.30, 1.0, 0.72, 1.0) if is_vert else Color(1.0, 0.78, 0.28, 1.0)) if is_sel else Color(0.30, 0.40, 0.48, 0.9)

		_diagram_canvas.draw_rect(r, fill_col, true)
		_diagram_canvas.draw_rect(r, border_col, false, 2.5 if is_sel else 1.3)

		_diagram_canvas.draw_string(font, r.position + Vector2(6, 17), stage["title"], HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, r.size.x - 10, 11, Color(0.92, 0.95, 0.98, 1.0))
		_diagram_canvas.draw_string(font, r.position + Vector2(6, 33), stage["sub"], HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, r.size.x - 10, 10, Color(0.65, 0.75, 0.82, 1.0))

		if has_node("/root/SimState"):
			var stats := SimState.get_node_stats(id)
			var vpp_str := "Vpp: %.2f V" % stats["v_pp"]
			var val_col := Color(0.35, 1.0, 0.75, 1.0) if is_vert else Color(1.0, 0.82, 0.35, 1.0)
			_diagram_canvas.draw_string(font, r.position + Vector2(6, 54), vpp_str, HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, r.size.x - 10, 11, val_col)

	# Draw Schematic CRT Tube Symbol on Far Right
	var crt_rect := Rect2(Vector2(sz.x * 0.83, sz.y * 0.56), Vector2(clampf(sz.x * 0.13, 95.0, 140.0), 86.0))
	_diagram_canvas.draw_rect(crt_rect, Color(0.10, 0.34, 0.42, 1.0) if power_on else Color(0.08, 0.14, 0.16, 1.0), true)
	_diagram_canvas.draw_rect(crt_rect, Color(0.55, 0.85, 0.92, 0.9), false, 2.0)
	_diagram_canvas.draw_string(font, crt_rect.position + Vector2(8, 22), "CRT TUBE", HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, crt_rect.size.x - 12, 11, Color.WHITE)
	_diagram_canvas.draw_string(font, crt_rect.position + Vector2(8, 40), "Y + X Plates", HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, crt_rect.size.x - 12, 10, Color(0.80, 0.95, 0.92, 1.0))

func _refresh_inspector() -> void:
	if not has_node("/root/SimState") or _title_lbl == null:
		return
	var sel_id := SimState.selected_circuit_node
	var active_stage := STAGES[0]
	for s in STAGES:
		if s["id"] == sel_id:
			active_stage = s
			break

	_title_lbl.text = active_stage["title"]
	_eq_lbl.text = "Equation:  " + active_stage["equation"]
	_desc_lbl.text = active_stage["desc"]

	var coup_name: String = AnalogScopeEngine.COUPLING_NAMES[SimState.analog_config["coupling"]]
	var probe_x: int = int(SimState.analog_config["probe_attenuation"])
	var trig_lvl: float = SimState.analog_config["trigger_level"]
	var v_pos: float = SimState.analog_config["vert_pos_div"]
	_params_lbl.text = "Active State:  Coupling = %s  |  Probe = %dx  |  Trig = %.2f V  |  V-Pos = %.1f div" % [
		coup_name, probe_x, trig_lvl, v_pos
	]

	var stats := SimState.get_node_stats(sel_id)
	_metrics_lbl.text = "Vpp: %.3f V   |   Vrms: %.3f V   |   Min/Max: %.2fV / %.2fV" % [
		stats["v_pp"], stats["v_rms"], stats["v_min"], stats["v_max"]
	]
	if _diagram_canvas != null:
		_diagram_canvas.queue_redraw()
	if _probe_canvas != null:
		_probe_canvas.queue_redraw()

func _draw_node_probe_waveform() -> void:
	var sz := _probe_canvas.size
	if sz.x < 20.0 or sz.y < 20.0:
		return
	_probe_canvas.draw_rect(Rect2(Vector2.ZERO, sz), Color(0.03, 0.05, 0.07, 1.0), true)
	_probe_canvas.draw_rect(Rect2(Vector2.ZERO, sz), Color(0.22, 0.34, 0.42, 1.0), false, 1.5)

	# Zero-volt midline
	var mid_y := sz.y * 0.5
	_probe_canvas.draw_line(Vector2(0.0, mid_y), Vector2(sz.x, mid_y), Color(0.20, 0.30, 0.36, 0.9), 1.0)

	if not has_node("/root/SimState"):
		return
	var stats := SimState.get_node_stats(SimState.selected_circuit_node)
	var buf: PackedFloat64Array = stats["buffer"]
	var n := buf.size()
	if n < 2:
		return

	# Auto-scale vertical preview cleanly around 0V so small attenuated or large rail signals remain clear
	var peak_abs := maxf(maxf(absf(stats["v_min"]), absf(stats["v_max"])) * 1.25, 0.5)
	var px_per_v := (sz.y * 0.46) / peak_abs
	var step_x := sz.x / float(n - 1)

	var pts := PackedVector2Array()
	pts.resize(n)
	for i in range(n):
		pts[i] = Vector2(float(i) * step_x, mid_y - float(buf[i]) * px_per_v)

	var is_trig_branch := (SimState.selected_circuit_node == "N7_TRIGGER" or SimState.selected_circuit_node == "N8_SWEEP")
	var trace_col := Color(1.0, 0.80, 0.30, 1.0) if is_trig_branch else Color(0.30, 1.0, 0.72, 1.0)
	_probe_canvas.draw_polyline(pts, trace_col, 2.0, true)

	var font := ThemeDB.fallback_font
	_probe_canvas.draw_string(font, Vector2(8, 16), "LIVE NODE PROBE (±%.2f V full-scale)" % peak_abs, HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 10, Color(0.75, 0.85, 0.90, 0.9))

class_name ChallengeManager

const MISSIONS := [

		"req_tip": "BB_TP2",

static func build_mission_instance(mission_idx: int) -> Dictionary:
	var base: Dictionary = MISSIONS[clampi(mission_idx, 0, MISSIONS.size() - 1)].duplicate(true)
	if base.get("randomize_fg", false):
		var rng := RandomNumberGenerator.new()
		rng.randomize()
		var amp_choices := [1.2, 1.5, 1.8, 2.0, 2.4, 2.5]
		var freq_choices := [500.0, 800.0, 1000.0, 1250.0, 2000.0, 2500.0]
		var off_choices := [-0.8, -0.4, 0.0, 0.4, 0.8]
		var amp: float = amp_choices[rng.randi_range(0, amp_choices.size() - 1)]
		var freq: float = freq_choices[rng.randi_range(0, freq_choices.size() - 1)]
		var off: float = off_choices[rng.randi_range(0, off_choices.size() - 1)]
		base["true_vpp"] = amp * 2.0
		base["true_freq"] = freq
		base["true_offset"] = off
		base["fg_amp"] = amp
		base["fg_wf"] = SignalGenerator.WaveformType.SINE
	elif base.get("procedural_bb", false):
		SimState.select_breadboard_model(3, true)
		var cfg := SimState.breadboard_config
		var omega := TAU * float(cfg["freq_hz"])
		var rc := float(cfg["r_ohm"]) * float(cfg["c_farad"])
		var gain := 1.0 / sqrt(1.0 + pow(omega * rc, 2.0))
		base["true_vpp"] = float(cfg["v_in_amp"]) * 2.0 * gain
		base["true_freq"] = float(cfg["freq_hz"])
		base["true_offset"] = 0.0
	elif base.has("bb_model"):
		SimState.select_breadboard_model(int(base["bb_model"]), false)
		if int(base["bb_model"]) == 0:
			var cfg := SimState.breadboard_config
			var omega := TAU * float(cfg["freq_hz"])
			var rc := float(cfg["r_ohm"]) * float(cfg["c_farad"])
			var gain := 1.0 / sqrt(1.0 + pow(omega * rc, 2.0))
			base["true_vpp"] = float(cfg["v_in_amp"]) * 2.0 * gain
			base["true_freq"] = float(cfg["freq_hz"])
			base["true_offset"] = 0.0
	return base

static func evaluate_submission(mission: Dictionary, sub_vpp: float, sub_freq: float, sub_offset: float, time_left: float) -> Dictionary:
	var p1_bnc := str(SimState.analog_config.get("probe1_bnc", "UNPLUGGED"))
	var p1_tip := str(SimState.analog_config.get("probe1_tip", "FLOATING"))
	var p2_bnc := str(SimState.analog_config.get("probe2_bnc", "UNPLUGGED"))
	var p2_tip := str(SimState.analog_config.get("probe2_tip", "FLOATING"))

	var req_tip := str(mission.get("req_tip", "CAL_1VPP"))
	var routing_ok := (p1_bnc == "CH1" and p1_tip == req_tip) or (p2_bnc == "CH1" and p2_tip == req_tip)

	var coupling_ok := int(SimState.analog_config["coupling"]) == AnalogScopeEngine.CouplingMode.DC
	var trig_ok := SimState.is_triggered
	var comp_val := float(SimState.analog_config["probe_compensation"])
	var comp_ok := absf(comp_val - 1.0) <= 0.07
	if mission.get("req_10x", false):
		comp_ok = comp_ok and (float(SimState.analog_config["probe_attenuation"]) > 1.5)

	var cal_score := 0
	if routing_ok:
		cal_score += 15
	if coupling_ok:
		cal_score += 10
	if trig_ok:
		cal_score += 10
	if comp_ok:
		cal_score += 15

	var true_vpp := float(mission["true_vpp"])
	var true_freq := float(mission["true_freq"])
	var true_off := float(mission["true_offset"])

	var vpp_err_pct := absf(sub_vpp - true_vpp) / maxf(true_vpp, 1e-6) * 100.0
	var freq_err_pct := absf(sub_freq - true_freq) / maxf(true_freq, 1e-6) * 100.0
	var off_err_v := absf(sub_offset - true_off)

	var vpp_ok := vpp_err_pct <= 8.0
	var freq_ok := freq_err_pct <= 8.0
	var off_ok := off_err_v <= 0.25

	var read_score := 0
	if vpp_ok:
		read_score += 20
	elif vpp_err_pct <= 16.0:
		read_score += 10
	if freq_ok:
		read_score += 20
	elif freq_err_pct <= 16.0:
		read_score += 10
	if off_ok:
		read_score += 10

	var total_score := cal_score + read_score
	var passed := (total_score >= 80) and (time_left > 0.0)

	return {
		"passed": passed,
		"total_score": total_score,
		"cal_score": cal_score,
		"read_score": read_score,
		"routing_ok": routing_ok,
		"coupling_ok": coupling_ok,
		"trig_ok": trig_ok,
		"comp_ok": comp_ok,
		"vpp_ok": vpp_ok,
		"freq_ok": freq_ok,
		"off_ok": off_ok,
		"true_vpp": true_vpp,
		"true_freq": true_freq,
		"true_offset": true_off,
		"vpp_err_pct": vpp_err_pct,
		"freq_err_pct": freq_err_pct,
		"off_err_v": off_err_v
	}

extends Control

var _canvas: WaveformCanvas
var _animate_check: CheckButton
var _power_led: ColorRect
var _pwr_btn: Button
var _workbench_3d: Workbench3D
var _instrument_view: Control
var _circuit_view: AnalogCircuitExplorer
var _challenge_view: ChallengePanel
var _btn_3d_mode: Button
var _btn_inst_mode: Button
var _btn_arch_mode: Button
var _btn_chal_mode: Button
var _popup_panel: PanelContainer
var _popup_timer_lbl: Label
var _popup_desc_lbl: Label
var _popup_vpp: SpinBox
var _popup_freq: SpinBox
var _popup_off: SpinBox
var _popup_res_lbl: Label

# Two-way synced 2D controls
var _knobs_2d := {}
var _slope_opt: OptionButton
var _coup_opt: OptionButton
var _probe_opt: OptionButton
var _wf_opt: OptionButton
var _gen_sliders := {}
var _syncing_ui := false

const CHASSIS_GREY := Color(0.77, 0.75, 0.72, 1.0)
const CHASSIS_DARK := Color(0.24, 0.23, 0.22, 1.0)
const BEZEL_CREAM := Color(0.85, 0.79, 0.69, 1.0)
const BEZEL_INNER := Color(0.68, 0.62, 0.53, 1.0)
const SILKSCREEN_INK := Color(0.14, 0.14, 0.13, 1.0)

const VOLTS_STEPS := [0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0]
const VOLTS_LABELS: Array[String] = ["0.2V", "0.5V", "1V", "2V", "5V"]
const TIME_STEPS := [0.0001, 0.0002, 0.0005, 0.001, 0.002, 0.005]
const TIME_LABELS: Array[String] = ["0.1ms", "0.2ms", "0.5ms", "1ms", "2ms", "5ms"]

func _ready() -> void:
	print("[Oscilloscope Lab] Milestone 4/5 Synced Boot | Renderer: ", RenderingServer.get_video_adapter_name())
	_build_workspace()
	SimState.state_updated.connect(func(_dt := 0.0): _sync_selector_states())
	SimState.state_updated.connect(_sync_2d_from_state)
	_sync_2d_from_state()

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	if _animate_check != null and _animate_check.button_pressed:
		SimState.advance_time(delta)

func _make_panel_style(bg: Color, border: Color, border_w := 2, radius := 4) -> StyleBoxFlat:
	var sb := StyleBoxFlat.new()
	sb.bg_color = bg
	sb.border_color = border
	sb.set_border_width_all(border_w)
	sb.set_corner_radius_all(radius)
	return sb

func _set_active_mode(mode_idx: int) -> void:
	var views: Array[Control] = [_workbench_3d, _instrument_view, _circuit_view, _challenge_view]
	for i in range(views.size()):
		var v := views[i]
		var active := (i == mode_idx)
		v.visible = active
		if active:
			v.modulate.a = 0.0
			var tw := create_tween()
			tw.tween_property(v, "modulate:a", 1.0, 0.18).set_trans(Tween.TRANS_CUBIC).set_ease(Tween.EASE_OUT)
	_btn_3d_mode.button_pressed = (mode_idx == 0)
	_btn_inst_mode.button_pressed = (mode_idx == 1)
	_btn_arch_mode.button_pressed = (mode_idx == 2)
	_btn_chal_mode.button_pressed = (mode_idx == 3)

func _sync_2d_from_state() -> void:
	if _syncing_ui:
		return
	_syncing_ui = true

	var on: bool = bool(SimState.view_config.get("power_on", true))
	if _pwr_btn != null:
		_pwr_btn.set_pressed_no_signal(on)
	if _power_led != null:
		_power_led.color = Color(1.0, 0.58, 0.12, 1.0) if on else Color(0.28, 0.20, 0.15, 1.0)

	# Sync Analog Knobs
	for key in ["intensity", "focus", "scale_illum", "trigger_level", "horiz_pos_div", "vert_pos_div", "persistence"]:
		if _knobs_2d.has(key):
			_knobs_2d[key].value = float(SimState.analog_config[key])

	# Sync Discrete Stepped Knobs (VOLTS/DIV and TIME/DIV)
	if _knobs_2d.has("volts_per_div"):
		var v_cur: float = float(SimState.view_config["volts_per_div"])
		var best_v := 2
		for i in range(VOLTS_STEPS.size()):
			if absf(VOLTS_STEPS[i] - v_cur) < 1e-4:
				best_v = i
				break
		_knobs_2d["volts_per_div"].value = float(best_v)

	if _knobs_2d.has("time_per_div"):
		var t_cur: float = float(SimState.view_config["time_per_div"])
		var best_t := 2
		for i in range(TIME_STEPS.size()):
			if absf(TIME_STEPS[i] - t_cur) < 1e-6:
				best_t = i
				break
		_knobs_2d["time_per_div"].value = float(best_t)

	# Sync OptionButtons
	if _slope_opt != null:
		_slope_opt.selected = int(SimState.analog_config["trigger_slope"])
	if _coup_opt != null:
		_coup_opt.selected = int(SimState.analog_config["coupling"])
	if _probe_opt != null:
		_probe_opt.selected = 1 if float(SimState.analog_config["probe_attenuation"]) > 1.5 else 0
	if _wf_opt != null:
		_wf_opt.selected = int(SimState.signal_params["waveform"])

	# Sync Bottom Function Generator Sliders
	for s_key in _gen_sliders.keys():
		var entry: Dictionary = _gen_sliders[s_key]
		var val: float = float(SimState.signal_params[s_key])
		var slider: HSlider = entry["slider"]
		var lbl: Label = entry["label"]
		slider.set_value_no_signal(val)
		lbl.text = "%.1f" % val

	_syncing_ui = false

func _build_workspace() -> void:

func _make_section_box(parent: Control, header_text: String) -> VBoxContainer:
	var panel := PanelContainer.new()
	panel.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", _make_panel_style(Color(0.74, 0.72, 0.69, 1.0), Color(0.32, 0.31, 0.30, 1.0), 1, 3))
	parent.add_child(panel)

	var margin := MarginContainer.new()
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_left", 6)
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_right", 6)
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_top", 4)
	margin.add_theme_constant_override("margin_bottom", 6)
	panel.add_child(margin)

	var vbox := VBoxContainer.new()
	vbox.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 4)
	margin.add_child(vbox)

	var lbl := _silk_label(header_text, 10)
	lbl.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	vbox.add_child(lbl)
	return vbox

func _silk_label(text: String, font_sz: int) -> Label:
	var lbl := Label.new()
	lbl.text = text
	lbl.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", font_sz)
	lbl.add_theme_color_override("font_color", SILKSCREEN_INK)
	return lbl

func _add_knob(parent: Control, param_key: String, label_txt: String, min_v: float, max_v: float, step_v: float, init_v: float, cap_col: Color, labels: Array[String], cb: Callable) -> void:
	var knob := RotaryKnob.new()
	knob.label_text = label_txt
	knob.min_value = min_v
	knob.max_value = max_v
	knob.step = step_v
	knob.value = init_v
	knob.accent_cap_color = cap_col
	knob.discrete_labels = labels
	knob.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	knob.value_changed.connect(cb)
	parent.add_child(knob)
	_knobs_2d[param_key] = knob

func _add_compact_slider(parent: Control, param_key: String, name_txt: String, min_v: float, max_v: float, step_v: float, init_v: float, cb: Callable) -> void:
	var box := HBoxContainer.new()
	box.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	box.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 4)
	parent.add_child(box)

	var lbl := Label.new()
	lbl.text = "%s:" % name_txt
	lbl.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 11)
	box.add_child(lbl)

	var val_lbl := Label.new()
	val_lbl.text = "%.1f" % init_v
	val_lbl.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 11)
	val_lbl.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color(0.45, 0.95, 0.78, 1.0))
	box.add_child(val_lbl)

	var s := HSlider.new()
	s.min_value = min_v
	s.max_value = max_v
	s.step = step_v
	s.value = init_v
	s.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	s.value_changed.connect(func(v: float) -> void:
		val_lbl.text = "%.1f" % v
		cb.call(v)
	)
	box.add_child(s)
	_gen_sliders[param_key] = {"slider": s, "label": val_lbl}


func _sync_selector_states() -> void:
	if SimState == null:
		return
	var mode_opt := find_child("ModeOption", true, false) as OptionButton
	if mode_opt:
		mode_opt.select(1 if SimState.app_mode == SimState.AppMode.CALIBRATION else 0)
	var theme_opt := find_child("ThemeOption", true, false) as OptionButton
	if theme_opt:
		for i in range(theme_opt.item_count):
			if theme_opt.get_item_metadata(i) == SimState.lab_theme:
				theme_opt.select(i)
				break
	var fg_ch_opt := find_child("FgChOption", true, false) as OptionButton
	if fg_ch_opt:
		var ch_idx := clampi(int(SimState.fg_active_ch) - 1, 0, fg_ch_opt.item_count - 1)
		fg_ch_opt.select(ch_idx)

func _ready() -> void:
	set_lab_mode("NORMAL")

func select_fg_channel(ch: int) -> void:
	fg_selected_channel = 1 if ch != 2 else 2
	state_updated.emit()

func set_lab_theme(idx: int) -> void:
	lab_theme_idx = clampi(idx, 0, LabEnvironmentBuilder.LAB_THEMES.size() - 1)
	state_updated.emit()

func set_board_is_pcb(is_pcb: bool) -> void:
	board_is_pcb = is_pcb
	state_updated.emit()

func select_breadboard_model(idx: int, randomize_proc := false) -> void:
	breadboard_model_idx = clampi(idx, 0, Breadboard3DBuilder.CIRCUIT_MODELS.size() - 1)
	breadboard_config = Breadboard3DBuilder.CIRCUIT_MODELS[breadboard_model_idx].duplicate(true)
	if randomize_proc or breadboard_config.get("id") == "PROCEDURAL_MIX":
		var rng := RandomNumberGenerator.new()
		rng.randomize()
		var r_list := [470.0, 1000.0, 2200.0, 4700.0]
		var c_list := [22e-9, 47e-9, 100e-9, 220e-9]
		var f_list := [500.0, 1000.0, 1500.0, 2500.0]
		var a_list := [1.5, 2.0, 2.5, 3.0]
		breadboard_config["r_ohm"] = r_list[rng.randi_range(0, r_list.size() - 1)]
		breadboard_config["c_farad"] = c_list[rng.randi_range(0, c_list.size() - 1)]
		breadboard_config["freq_hz"] = f_list[rng.randi_range(0, f_list.size() - 1)]
		breadboard_config["v_in_amp"] = a_list[rng.randi_range(0, a_list.size() - 1)]
	if str(analog_config.get("probe1_tip", "")) in ["FG_CH1", "FLOATING"]:
		analog_config["probe1_bnc"] = "CH1"
		analog_config["probe1_tip"] = "BB_TP1"
	if str(analog_config.get("probe2_tip", "")) in ["FG_CH2", "FLOATING"]:
		analog_config["probe2_bnc"] = "CH2"
		analog_config["probe2_tip"] = "BB_TP2"
	recompute()

func set_lab_mode(mode_name: String) -> void:
	lab_mode = mode_name
	if mode_name == "CALIBRATION":
		view_config["power_on"] = true
		view_config["volts_per_div"] = 5.0
		view_config["ch2_volts_per_div"] = 5.0
		view_config["time_per_div"] = 0.005
		analog_config["vert_mode"] = 0
		analog_config["coupling"] = AnalogScopeEngine.CouplingMode.GND
		analog_config["ch2_coupling"] = AnalogScopeEngine.CouplingMode.GND
		analog_config["probe_attenuation"] = 1.0
		analog_config["probe_compensation"] = 0.65
		analog_config["probe1_bnc"] = "UNPLUGGED"
		analog_config["probe1_tip"] = "FLOATING"
		analog_config["probe2_bnc"] = "UNPLUGGED"
		analog_config["probe2_tip"] = "FLOATING"
		analog_config["vert_pos_div"] = 3.2
		analog_config["ch2_vert_pos_div"] = -3.0
		analog_config["horiz_pos_div"] = -3.5
		analog_config["trigger_level"] = 3.6
		analog_config["intensity"] = 0.45
		analog_config["focus"] = 0.40
	else:
		view_config["power_on"] = true
		view_config["volts_per_div"] = 1.0
		view_config["ch2_volts_per_div"] = 1.0
		view_config["time_per_div"] = 0.0005
		analog_config["vert_mode"] = 1 # ALT (Both CH1 & CH2 active)
		analog_config["coupling"] = AnalogScopeEngine.CouplingMode.DC
		analog_config["ch2_coupling"] = AnalogScopeEngine.CouplingMode.DC
		analog_config["probe_attenuation"] = 1.0
		analog_config["probe_compensation"] = 1.0
		analog_config["probe1_bnc"] = "CH1"
		analog_config["probe1_tip"] = "FG_CH1"
		analog_config["probe2_bnc"] = "CH2"
		analog_config["probe2_tip"] = "FG_CH2"
		analog_config["vert_pos_div"] = 1.2
		analog_config["ch2_vert_pos_div"] = -1.5
		analog_config["horiz_pos_div"] = 0.0
		analog_config["trigger_level"] = 0.0
		analog_config["intensity"] = 0.85
		analog_config["focus"] = 0.88
	recompute()

func set_signal_param(key: String, value: Variant) -> void:
	var ch_dict: Dictionary = fg_channels[fg_selected_channel]
	if ch_dict.get(key) == value:
		return
	ch_dict[key] = value
	recompute()

func set_view_param(key: String, value: Variant) -> void:
	if view_config.get(key) == value:
		return
	view_config[key] = value
	recompute()

func set_analog_param(key: String, value: Variant) -> void:
	if analog_config.get(key) == value:
		return
	analog_config[key] = value
	recompute()

func select_circuit_node(node_id: String) -> void:
	if selected_circuit_node == node_id:
		return
	selected_circuit_node = node_id
	selected_node_changed.emit(node_id)
	state_updated.emit()

func advance_time(delta: float) -> void:
	if not view_config["running"] or not view_config["power_on"]:
		return
	sim_time_offset = fmod(sim_time_offset + delta * 0.05, 10.0)
	recompute()

func _resolve_tip_signal(tip_tgt: String) -> Dictionary:
	if tip_tgt == "FG_CH1" and bool(fg_channels[1].get("enabled", true)):
		return fg_channels[1]
	elif tip_tgt == "FG_CH2" and bool(fg_channels[2].get("enabled", true)):
		return fg_channels[2]
	elif tip_tgt == "CAL_1VPP":
		return {
			"waveform": SignalGenerator.WaveformType.SQUARE,
			"amplitude": 0.5,
			"frequency": 1000.0,
			"phase_deg": 0.0,
			"dc_offset": 0.5,
			"duty_cycle": 0.50,
			"noise_rms": 0.004,
			"noise_seed": 42
		}
	return {
		"waveform": SignalGenerator.WaveformType.DC,
		"amplitude": 0.0,
		"frequency": 50.0,
		"phase_deg": 0.0,
		"dc_offset": 0.0,
		"duty_cycle": 0.50,
		"noise_rms": 0.012,
		"noise_seed": 77
	}

func recompute() -> void:
	var sweep_span: float = float(view_config["time_per_div"]) * 10.0
	var sweep_samples: int = int(view_config["sample_count"])
	var total_span := sweep_span * 3.0
	var total_samples := sweep_samples * 3

	var p1_bnc: String = str(analog_config.get("probe1_bnc", "CH1"))
	var p1_tip: String = str(analog_config.get("probe1_tip", "FG_CH1"))
	var p2_bnc: String = str(analog_config.get("probe2_bnc", "CH2"))
	var p2_tip: String = str(analog_config.get("probe2_tip", "FG_CH2"))

	var ch1_tip := "FLOATING"
	if p1_bnc == "CH1":
		ch1_tip = p1_tip
	elif p2_bnc == "CH1":
		ch1_tip = p2_tip

	var ch2_tip := "FLOATING"
	if p2_bnc == "CH2":
		ch2_tip = p2_tip
	elif p1_bnc == "CH2":
		ch2_tip = p1_tip

	var psu_on := bool(analog_config.get("psu_power_on", true))
	var psu_vcc := clampf(float(analog_config.get("psu_voltage_coarse", 12.0)) + float(analog_config.get("psu_voltage_fine", 0.0)), 0.0, 30.0) if psu_on else 0.0
	breadboard_config["psu_vcc"] = psu_vcc

	# CH 1 Analog Chain
	var raw1 := Breadboard3DBuilder.generate_breadboard_signal(breadboard_config, ch1_tip, total_samples, total_span, sim_time_offset) if ch1_tip.begins_with("BB_TP") else SignalGenerator.generate_buffer(_resolve_tip_signal(ch1_tip), total_samples, total_span, sim_time_offset)
	raw_time_buffer = raw1["times"]
	raw_voltage_buffer = raw1["voltages"]
	var dt: float = raw1["dt"]

	var analog1 := AnalogScopeEngine.process_chain(raw_time_buffer, raw_voltage_buffer, dt, analog_config, view_config)
	analog_nodes = analog1["nodes"]
	time_buffer = analog1["sweep_times"]
	voltage_buffer = analog1["sweep_volts"]
	is_triggered = analog1["triggered"]

	# CH 2 Analog Chain
	var raw2 := Breadboard3DBuilder.generate_breadboard_signal(breadboard_config, ch2_tip, total_samples, total_span, sim_time_offset) if ch2_tip.begins_with("BB_TP") else SignalGenerator.generate_buffer(_resolve_tip_signal(ch2_tip), total_samples, total_span, sim_time_offset)
	var cfg2 := analog_config.duplicate()
	cfg2["coupling"] = analog_config.get("ch2_coupling", AnalogScopeEngine.CouplingMode.DC)
	cfg2["vert_pos_div"] = analog_config.get("ch2_vert_pos_div", 0.0)
	cfg2["probe_attenuation"] = analog_config.get("probe2_attenuation", 1.0)
	var view2 := view_config.duplicate()
	view2["volts_per_div"] = view_config.get("ch2_volts_per_div", 1.0)
	var analog2 := AnalogScopeEngine.process_chain(raw2["times"], raw2["voltages"], dt, cfg2, view2)
	ch2_voltage_buffer = analog2["sweep_volts"]

	state_updated.emit()

func get_calibration_checklist() -> Array[Dictionary]:
	var p1_bnc: String = str(analog_config.get("probe1_bnc", "UNPLUGGED"))
	var p1_tip: String = str(analog_config.get("probe1_tip", "FLOATING"))
	var p2_bnc: String = str(analog_config.get("probe2_bnc", "UNPLUGGED"))
	var p2_tip: String = str(analog_config.get("probe2_tip", "FLOATING"))

	var step1_ok := (p1_bnc == "CH1" and p1_tip == "CAL_1VPP") or (p2_bnc == "CH1" and p2_tip == "CAL_1VPP")
	var step2_ok := (int(analog_config["coupling"]) == AnalogScopeEngine.CouplingMode.DC) and (absf(float(analog_config["vert_pos_div"])) <= 0.45)
	var trig_v := float(analog_config["trigger_level"])
	var step3_ok := is_triggered and (trig_v >= 0.15 and trig_v <= 0.85)
	var v_div := float(view_config["volts_per_div"])
	var t_div := float(view_config["time_per_div"])
	var step4_ok := (v_div <= 1.0) and (t_div >= 0.0002 and t_div <= 0.001)
	var comp := float(analog_config["probe_compensation"])
	var step5_ok := (float(analog_config["probe_attenuation"]) > 1.5) and (absf(comp - 1.0) <= 0.06)

	return [
		{"done": step1_ok, "text": "1. Plug Probe BNC into CH 1 & clip Tip onto CAL 1Vp-p"},
		{"done": step2_ok, "text": "2. Set CH 1 Coupling = DC & center V-Position (~0 div)"},
		{"done": step3_ok, "text": "3. Adjust Trigger LEVEL (0.2V–0.8V) to lock square wave"},
		{"done": step4_ok, "text": "4. Set VOLTS/DIV (0.5V/1V) & TIME/DIV (0.5ms) to frame wave"},
		{"done": step5_ok, "text": "5. Flip Probe to 10x & tune PROBE COMP for flat corners"}
	]

func get_node_stats(node_id: String) -> Dictionary:
	var buf: PackedFloat64Array = analog_nodes.get(node_id, PackedFloat64Array())
	if buf.is_empty():
		return {"v_min": 0.0, "v_max": 0.0, "v_pp": 0.0, "v_rms": 0.0, "v_inst": 0.0, "buffer": buf}
	var v_min := buf[0]
	var v_max := buf[0]
	var sum_sq := 0.0
	for v in buf:
		if v < v_min:
			v_min = v
		if v > v_max:
			v_max = v
		sum_sq += v * v
	return {
		"v_min": v_min,
		"v_max": v_max,
		"v_pp": v_max - v_min,
		"v_rms": sqrt(sum_sq / float(buf.size())),
		"v_inst": buf[buf.size() / 2],
		"buffer": buf
	}

FILE: scripts/signal/signal_generator.gd
========================================

class_name SignalGenerator
extends RefCounted

enum WaveformType {
	SINE,
	SQUARE,
	TRIANGLE,
	SAWTOOTH,
	PULSE,
	DC,
	NOISE
}

const WAVEFORM_NAMES := [
	"Sine",
	"Square",
	"Triangle",
	"Sawtooth",
	"Pulse",
	"DC",
	"Noise"
]

static func evaluate_sample(
	t: float,
	waveform: int,
	amplitude: float,
	frequency: float,
	phase_rad: float,
	dc_offset: float,
	duty_cycle: float,
	noise_rms: float,
	rng: RandomNumberGenerator = null
) -> float:
	var v_base := 0.0
	var clamped_duty := clampf(duty_cycle, 0.01, 0.99)
	var phase_cycles := fposmod(frequency * t + (phase_rad / TAU), 1.0)

	match waveform:
		WaveformType.SINE:
			v_base = dc_offset + amplitude * sin(TAU * frequency * t + phase_rad)
		WaveformType.SQUARE:
			v_base = dc_offset + (amplitude if phase_cycles < clamped_duty else -amplitude)
		WaveformType.TRIANGLE:
			if phase_cycles < 0.5:
				v_base = dc_offset + lerpf(-amplitude, amplitude, phase_cycles * 2.0)
			else:
				v_base = dc_offset + lerpf(amplitude, -amplitude, (phase_cycles - 0.5) * 2.0)
		WaveformType.SAWTOOTH:
			v_base = dc_offset + lerpf(-amplitude, amplitude, phase_cycles)
		WaveformType.PULSE:
			v_base = dc_offset + (amplitude if phase_cycles < clamped_duty else 0.0)
		WaveformType.DC:
			v_base = dc_offset
		WaveformType.NOISE:
			v_base = dc_offset

	if noise_rms > 0.0 and rng != null:
		v_base += rng.randfn(0.0, noise_rms)

	return v_base

static func generate_buffer(params: Dictionary, sample_count: int, time_span: float, start_time: float = 0.0) -> Dictionary:
	var count := maxi(sample_count, 2)
	var times := PackedFloat64Array()
	var voltages := PackedFloat64Array()
	times.resize(count)
	voltages.resize(count)

	var waveform: int = params.get("waveform", WaveformType.SINE)
	var amplitude: float = params.get("amplitude", 1.0)
	var frequency: float = params.get("frequency", 1000.0)
	var phase_deg: float = params.get("phase_deg", 0.0)
	var dc_offset: float = params.get("dc_offset", 0.0)
	var duty_cycle: float = params.get("duty_cycle", 0.5)
	var noise_rms: float = params.get("noise_rms", 0.0)
	var seed_val: int = params.get("noise_seed", 42)

	var rng := RandomNumberGenerator.new()
	rng.seed = seed_val
	var phase_rad := deg_to_rad(phase_deg)
	var dt := time_span / float(count - 1)

	for i in range(count):
		var t := start_time + float(i) * dt
		times[i] = t
		voltages[i] = evaluate_sample(
			t, waveform, amplitude, frequency, phase_rad, dc_offset, duty_cycle, noise_rms, rng
		)

	return {
		"times": times,
		"voltages": voltages,
		"dt": dt
	}

class_name ChallengePanel
extends PanelContainer

var time_remaining := 120.0

func _ready() -> void:
	var sb := StyleBoxFlat.new()
	sb.bg_color = Color(0.08, 0.10, 0.13, 1.0)
	sb.border_color = Color(0.25, 0.32, 0.40, 1.0)
	sb.set_border_width_all(2)
	sb.set_corner_radius_all(6)
	add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", sb)

	_build_ui()
	_select_mission(0, false)
	if has_node("/root/SimState"):
		SimState.state_updated.connect(_refresh_hardware_status)
	_refresh_hardware_status()
	mission_started.emit(active_mission)

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	if not is_running:
		return
	time_remaining = maxf(time_remaining - delta, 0.0)
	SimState.active_time_remaining = time_remaining
	_update_timer_display()
	if time_remaining <= 0.0:
		is_running = false
		_on_submit_pressed()

	# Graded Report Output
	var rep_panel := PanelContainer.new()
	rep_panel.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	var r_sb := StyleBoxFlat.new()
	r_sb.bg_color = Color(0.06, 0.08, 0.10, 1.0)
	r_sb.border_color = Color(0.24, 0.30, 0.38, 1.0)
	r_sb.set_border_width_all(1)
	r_sb.set_corner_radius_all(4)
	rep_panel.add_theme_stylebox_override("panel", r_sb)
	right_vbox.add_child(rep_panel)

	var rep_pad := MarginContainer.new()
	rep_pad.add_theme_constant_override("margin_left", 10)
	rep_pad.add_theme_constant_override("margin_right", 10)
	rep_pad.add_theme_constant_override("margin_top", 8)
	rep_pad.add_theme_constant_override("margin_bottom", 8)
	rep_panel.add_child(rep_pad)

	_result_lbl = RichTextLabel.new()
	_result_lbl.bbcode_enabled = true
	_result_lbl.text = "[color=#9ab0c4]Select a mission and click [b]▶ START MISSION[/b]. The oscilloscope will scramble so you can calibrate it in 3D, measure the waveform on the CRT graticule, and submit your readings here.[/color]"
	rep_pad.add_child(_result_lbl)

func _add_spin_row(grid: GridContainer, label_txt: String, min_v: float, max_v: float, step_v: float, init_v: float) -> SpinBox:
	var lbl := Label.new()
	lbl.text = label_txt
	lbl.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 12)
	lbl.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	grid.add_child(lbl)

	var sb := SpinBox.new()
	sb.min_value = min_v
	sb.max_value = max_v
	sb.step = step_v
	sb.value = init_v
	sb.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(130, 0)
	grid.add_child(sb)
	return sb

func _select_mission(idx: int, auto_start: bool) -> void:
	active_mission = ChallengeManager.build_mission_instance(idx)
	time_remaining = float(active_mission["time_limit"])
	is_running = auto_start
	_desc_lbl.text = str(active_mission["description"])
	_update_timer_display()

func _on_start_mission() -> void:
	var idx := _mission_opt.selected
	_select_mission(idx, true)
	SimState.active_challenge_running = true
	SimState.active_mission_data = active_mission
	SimState.active_time_remaining = time_remaining
	SimState.last_mission_report = ""
	SimState.submission_attempts = 0
	SimState.failed_attempts = 0
	SimState.set_lab_mode("CALIBRATION")
	if active_mission.get("randomize_fg", false):
		SimState.select_fg_channel(1)
		SimState.set_signal_param("waveform", int(active_mission["fg_wf"]))
		SimState.set_signal_param("amplitude", float(active_mission["fg_amp"]))
		SimState.set_signal_param("frequency", float(active_mission["true_freq"]))
		SimState.set_signal_param("dc_offset", float(active_mission["true_offset"]))
	_result_lbl.text = "[color=#ffd166]MISSION ACTIVE![/color] Jump to [b][1] 3D Lab Workbench[/b] to plug probes, calibrate the VP-5231A, count CRT divisions, and return here to submit before time runs out."
	_refresh_hardware_status()

func _on_submit_pressed() -> void:
	var res := ChallengeManager.evaluate_submission(
		active_mission, _in_vpp.value, _in_freq.value, _in_offset.value, time_remaining
	)
	SimState.submission_attempts += 1
	if not res["passed"]:
		SimState.failed_attempts += 1
	if res["passed"] or time_remaining <= 0.0:
		is_running = false
		SimState.active_challenge_running = false
	_update_timer_display()
	var verdict := "[color=#4ade80][b]PASS — LAB PRACTICUM VERIFIED![/b][/color]" if res["passed"] else "[color=#f87171][b]NEEDS RE-CALIBRATION / RE-MEASUREMENT[/b][/color]"
	var lines: Array[String] = [
		"%s   |   [b]Score: %d / 100[/b] (Cal: %d/50, Reading: %d/50)" % [verdict, res["total_score"], res["cal_score"], res["read_score"]],
		"• Probe Routing: %s   |   DC Coupling: %s   |   Trigger Lock: %s   |   Probe Comp: %s" % [
			"OK" if res["routing_ok"] else "WRONG",
			"OK" if res["coupling_ok"] else "WRONG",
			"LOCKED" if res["trig_ok"] else "UNLOCKED",
			"OK" if res["comp_ok"] else "OFF"
		],
		"• Vpp: submitted [b]%.2f V[/b] vs true [b]%.2f V[/b] (err %.1f%%) — %s" % [_in_vpp.value, res["true_vpp"], res["vpp_err_pct"], "PASS" if res["vpp_ok"] else "FAIL"],
		"• Freq: submitted [b]%.0f Hz[/b] vs true [b]%.0f Hz[/b] (err %.1f%%) — %s" % [_in_freq.value, res["true_freq"], res["freq_err_pct"], "PASS" if res["freq_ok"] else "FAIL"],
		"• Offset: submitted [b]%.2f V[/b] vs true [b]%.2f V[/b] (err %.2f V) — %s" % [_in_offset.value, res["true_offset"], res["off_err_v"], "PASS" if res["off_ok"] else "FAIL"]
	]
	_last_summary = "Score: %d/100 (%s)" % [res["total_score"], "PASS" if res["passed"] else "RETRY"]
	if res["passed"] or time_remaining <= 0.0:
		SimState.last_mission_report = "FINAL: %d/100 (%s) | Attempts: %d (Fails: %d) | True: %.2fVpp, %.0fHz, %.2fV" % [res["total_score"], "PASS" if res["passed"] else "TIME UP", SimState.submission_attempts, SimState.failed_attempts, res["true_vpp"], res["true_freq"], res["true_offset"]]
	else:
		SimState.last_mission_report = "Score: %d/100 — KEEP GOING! | Attempts: %d (Fails: %d) | Route:%s | Comp:%s | Trig:%s | Vpp:%s | Freq:%s" % [res["total_score"], SimState.submission_attempts, SimState.failed_attempts, "OK" if res["routing_ok"] else "NO", "OK" if res["comp_ok"] else "OFF", "OK" if res["trig_ok"] else "NO", "OK" if res["vpp_ok"] else "ERR", "OK" if res["freq_ok"] else "ERR"]
	_result_lbl.text = "\n".join(lines)

func _update_timer_display() -> void:
	var mins := int(time_remaining) / 60
	var secs := int(time_remaining) % 60
	var state_tag := "RUNNING" if is_running else "STOPPED"
	_timer_lbl.text = "MISSION TIMER:  %02d:%02d  [%s]" % [mins, secs, state_tag]
	if time_remaining <= 20.0 and is_running:
		_timer_lbl.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color(1.0, 0.32, 0.30, 1.0))
	else:
		_timer_lbl.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color(1.0, 0.82, 0.28, 1.0))

func _refresh_hardware_status() -> void:
	if _status_routing == null or not has_node("/root/SimState"):
		return
	var p1_b := str(SimState.analog_config.get("probe1_bnc", "UNPLUGGED"))
	var p1_t := str(SimState.analog_config.get("probe1_tip", "FLOATING"))
	_status_routing.text = "Probe 1 Route: %s → %s" % [p1_b, p1_t]
	_status_routing.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color(0.45, 0.95, 0.72) if p1_b == "CH1" and p1_t != "FLOATING" else Color(0.95, 0.55, 0.45))

	var coup_names := ["DC", "AC", "GND"]
	var c_idx := int(SimState.analog_config.get("coupling", 0))
	_status_coupling.text = "CH 1 Coupling: %s" % coup_names[clampi(c_idx, 0, 2)]
	_status_coupling.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color(0.45, 0.95, 0.72) if c_idx == 0 else Color(0.95, 0.78, 0.35))

	_status_trig.text = "Trigger: %s (%.2f V)" % ["LOCKED" if SimState.is_triggered else "UNLOCKED", float(SimState.analog_config["trigger_level"])]
	_status_trig.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color(0.45, 0.95, 0.72) if SimState.is_triggered else Color(0.95, 0.45, 0.42))

	var comp := float(SimState.analog_config["probe_compensation"])
	var atten := int(round(float(SimState.analog_config["probe_attenuation"])))
	_status_comp.text = "Probe: %dx | Comp: %.2f" % [atten, comp]
	_status_comp.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color(0.45, 0.95, 0.72) if absf(comp - 1.0) <= 0.07 else Color(0.95, 0.78, 0.35))

var _last_summary := ""

func submit_external(vpp_v: float, freq_hz: float, off_v: float) -> void:
	_in_vpp.value = vpp_v
	_in_freq.value = freq_hz
	_in_offset.value = off_v
	_on_submit_pressed()

func get_last_summary_line() -> String:
	return _last_summary

========================================
FILE: scripts/ui/rotary_knob.gd
========================================

class_name RotaryKnob
extends Control

signal value_changed(new_value: float)

@export var label_text := "KNOB"
@export var min_value := 0.0
@export var max_value := 1.0
@export var step := 0.05
@export var value := 0.5
@export var accent_cap_color := Color(0.22, 0.21, 0.20, 1.0) # Charcoal or beige/red variable cap
@export var discrete_labels: Array[String] = []

var _dragging := false
var _drag_start_y := 0.0
var _drag_start_val := 0.0

func _ready() -> void:
	custom_minimum_size = Vector2(76, 84)
	mouse_default_cursor_shape = Control.CURSOR_POINTING_HAND

func set_knob_value(v: float, emit := false) -> void:
	var snapped_v := snappedf(clampf(v, min_value, max_value), step)
	if is_equal_approx(snapped_v, value):
		return
	value = snapped_v
	queue_redraw()
	if emit:
		value_changed.emit(value)

func _gui_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if event is InputEventMouseButton:
		var mb := event as InputEventMouseButton
		if mb.button_index == MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT:
			if mb.pressed:
				_dragging = true
				_drag_start_y = mb.position.y
				_drag_start_val = value
			else:
				_dragging = false
		elif mb.pressed and mb.button_index == MOUSE_BUTTON_WHEEL_UP:
			set_knob_value(value + step, true)
		elif mb.pressed and mb.button_index == MOUSE_BUTTON_WHEEL_DOWN:
			set_knob_value(value - step, true)
	elif event is InputEventMouseMotion and _dragging:
		var dy := _drag_start_y - (event as InputEventMouseMotion).position.y
		var span := maxf(max_value - min_value, 1e-6)
		var delta_val := (dy / 110.0) * span
		set_knob_value(_drag_start_val + delta_val, true)

func _draw() -> void:
	var font := ThemeDB.fallback_font
	var w := size.x
	var center := Vector2(w * 0.5, 42.0)
	var outer_r := 21.0

	# Top silk-screen label
	draw_string(font, Vector2(0, 11), label_text, HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER, w, 10, Color(0.14, 0.14, 0.13, 1.0))

	# Outer dial ticks (-135 deg to +135 deg)
	var start_ang := deg_to_rad(-225.0)
	var end_ang := deg_to_rad(45.0)
	var tick_count := discrete_labels.size() if discrete_labels.size() > 1 else 11
	for i in range(tick_count):
		var frac := float(i) / float(maxi(tick_count - 1, 1))
		var ang := lerpf(start_ang, end_ang, frac)
		var dir := Vector2(cos(ang), sin(ang))
		draw_line(center + dir * (outer_r + 2.0), center + dir * (outer_r + 6.0), Color(0.18, 0.18, 0.17, 0.9), 1.3)

	# Skirt ring + shadow
	draw_circle(center + Vector2(1.5, 2.5), outer_r + 1.0, Color(0.08, 0.08, 0.08, 0.35))
	draw_circle(center, outer_r, Color(0.78, 0.76, 0.72, 1.0))
	draw_arc(center, outer_r, 0.0, TAU, 32, Color(0.22, 0.22, 0.21, 1.0), 1.2)

	# Fluted charcoal body
	var body_r := outer_r - 3.5
	draw_circle(center, body_r, Color(0.17, 0.16, 0.16, 1.0))
	for f in range(12):
		var fa := float(f) * (TAU / 12.0)
		var fdir := Vector2(cos(fa), sin(fa))
		draw_line(center + fdir * (body_r - 2.5), center + fdir * body_r, Color(0.32, 0.31, 0.30, 0.9), 1.5)

	# Inner cap
	draw_circle(center, body_r * 0.56, accent_cap_color)
	draw_arc(center, body_r * 0.56, 0.0, TAU, 24, Color(0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.8), 1.0)

	# Pointer indicator line
	var norm := clampf((value - min_value) / maxf(max_value - min_value, 1e-6), 0.0, 1.0)
	var cur_ang := lerpf(start_ang, end_ang, norm)
	var p_dir := Vector2(cos(cur_ang), sin(cur_ang))
	draw_line(center + p_dir * 3.0, center + p_dir * (outer_r - 1.0), Color(0.96, 0.95, 0.92, 1.0), 2.2)

	# Bottom readout value
	var readout := ""
	if discrete_labels.size() > 0:
		var idx := clampi(int(round(norm * float(discrete_labels.size() - 1))), 0, discrete_labels.size() - 1)
		readout = discrete_labels[idx]
	else:
		readout = "%.2f" % value
	draw_string(font, Vector2(0, size.y - 4.0), readout, HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER, w, 10, Color(0.12, 0.15, 0.18, 1.0))

class_name Breadboard3DBuilder
extends RefCounted

const CIRCUIT_MODELS := [
	{
		"id": "RC_LPF",
		"name": "Model 1: RC Low-Pass Filter (1kΩ + 100nF)",
		"r_ohm": 1000.0,
		"c_farad": 100e-9,
		"v_in_amp": 2.5,
		"freq_hz": 1600.0,
		"summary": "Measure TP1 (Input) vs TP2 (Capacitor Out) to find cutoff attenuation and phase lag."
	},
	{
		"id": "DIODE_RECT",
		"name": "Model 2: Diode Rectifier & Ripple Filter",
		"v_in_amp": 3.0,
		"freq_hz": 500.0,
		"ripple_v": 0.45,
		"summary": "Probe TP1 (AC), TP2 (Half-Wave Rectified), and TP3 (Filtered DC Ripple)."
	},

		"name": "Model 3: NE555 Astable + ÷2 Clock Divider",
		"vcc": 5.0,

		"v_in_amp": 2.0,
		"freq_hz": 1200.0,

	}
]

static func build_breadboard_on_bench(parent: Node, snap_targets: Dictionary, interactables: Array) -> Dictionary:
	var root := Node3D.new()
	root.name = "BreadboardUnit"
	root.position = Vector3(0.0, 0.003, 0.255)
	parent.add_child(root)

	# 1. Main Board Substrate (switches between High-Contrast Cream Breadboard & Green FR-4 PCB)
	var body := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var box := BoxMesh.new()
	box.size = Vector3(0.165, 0.008, 0.076)
	body.mesh = box
	body.position = Vector3(0.0, 0.004, 0.0)
	var body_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	body_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.95, 0.94, 0.90, 1.0)
	body_mat.roughness = 0.48
	body.material_override = body_mat
	root.add_child(body)

	# Dark Beveled Perimeter Frame for crisp contrast against blue ESD mat
	var frame := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var f_box := BoxMesh.new()
	f_box.size = Vector3(0.171, 0.005, 0.082)
	frame.mesh = f_box
	frame.position = Vector3(0.0, 0.0025, 0.0)
	var f_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	f_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.12, 0.14, 0.17, 1.0)
	f_mat.roughness = 0.65
	frame.material_override = f_mat
	root.add_child(frame)

	# Group A: Breadboard-only tie-point holes & power stripes
	var bb_details := Node3D.new()
	root.add_child(bb_details)

	var groove := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var g_box := BoxMesh.new()
	g_box.size = Vector3(0.156, 0.0014, 0.009)
	groove.mesh = g_box
	groove.position = Vector3(0.0, 0.0082, 0.0)
	var g_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	g_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.58, 0.57, 0.54, 1.0)
	groove.material_override = g_mat
	bb_details.add_child(groove)

	for side in [-1.0, 1.0]:
		var red_line := MeshInstance3D.new()
		var r_box := BoxMesh.new()
		r_box.size = Vector3(0.152, 0.0008, 0.0018)
		red_line.mesh = r_box
		red_line.position = Vector3(0.0, 0.0083, side * 0.033)
		var r_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
		r_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.90, 0.14, 0.14, 1.0)
		red_line.material_override = r_mat
		bb_details.add_child(red_line)

		var blue_line := MeshInstance3D.new()
		blue_line.mesh = r_box
		blue_line.position = Vector3(0.0, 0.0083, side * 0.028)
		var b_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
		b_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.10, 0.36, 0.90, 1.0)
		blue_line.material_override = b_mat
		bb_details.add_child(blue_line)

	# High-contrast dark tie-point hole grid (22 columns x 6 rows)
	var hole_mesh := BoxMesh.new()
	hole_mesh.size = Vector3(0.0022, 0.0008, 0.0022)
	var hole_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	hole_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.14, 0.15, 0.17, 1.0)
	for col in range(-10, 11):
		var hx := float(col) * 0.0070
		for row_z in [-0.021, -0.015, -0.009, 0.009, 0.015, 0.021]:
			var h := MeshInstance3D.new()
			h.mesh = hole_mesh
			h.position = Vector3(hx, 0.0082, row_z)
			h.material_override = hole_mat
			bb_details.add_child(h)

	# Group B: Green FR-4 PCB Copper Traces & Silkscreen (visible when PCB mode selected)
	var pcb_details := Node3D.new()
	pcb_details.visible = false
	root.add_child(pcb_details)

	var trace_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	trace_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.95, 0.78, 0.24, 1.0)
	trace_mat.metallic = 0.85
	trace_mat.roughness = 0.22
	trace_mat.emission_enabled = true
	trace_mat.emission = Color(0.35, 0.28, 0.05, 1.0)
	trace_mat.emission_energy_multiplier = 0.45

	var trace_segments := [
		# Signal path: TP1 -> R1 -> TP2 -> C1/D1 -> TP3
		[Vector3(-0.056, 0.0083, 0.018), Vector3(-0.056, 0.0083, -0.012), 0.0026],
		[Vector3(-0.056, 0.0083, -0.012), Vector3(-0.020, 0.0083, -0.012), 0.0026],
		[Vector3(-0.020, 0.0083, -0.012), Vector3(-0.020, 0.0083, 0.018), 0.0026],
		[Vector3(-0.020, 0.0083, -0.012), Vector3(0.018, 0.0083, -0.012), 0.0026],
		[Vector3(0.018, 0.0083, -0.012), Vector3(0.018, 0.0083, 0.018), 0.0026],
		# Connected Ground Return Bus: Diode/Shunt leg -> TP4 GND -> Bottom GND Rail
		[Vector3(0.018, 0.0083, -0.012), Vector3(0.056, 0.0083, -0.012), 0.0028],
		[Vector3(0.056, 0.0083, -0.012), Vector3(0.056, 0.0083, 0.028), 0.0030],
		[Vector3(-0.068, 0.0083, 0.028), Vector3(0.068, 0.0083, 0.028), 0.0032],
		# Top VCC Power Rail from PSU +12V Pad (x=0.072, z=-0.028) to IC VCC Pin
		[Vector3(-0.020, 0.0083, -0.028), Vector3(0.072, 0.0083, -0.028), 0.0030],
		[Vector3(-0.020, 0.0083, -0.028), Vector3(-0.020, 0.0083, -0.006), 0.0024]
	]
	for seg in trace_segments:
		var p_a: Vector3 = seg[0]
		var p_b: Vector3 = seg[1]
		var w: float = seg[2]
		var t_mesh := MeshInstance3D.new()
		var t_box := BoxMesh.new()
		var span := p_b - p_a
		t_box.size = Vector3(maxf(absf(span.x), w), 0.0008, maxf(absf(span.z), w))
		t_mesh.mesh = t_box
		t_mesh.position = (p_a + p_b) * 0.5
		t_mesh.material_override = trace_mat
		pcb_details.add_child(t_mesh)

	# Per-Model 3D Electronic Component Assemblies (Model 1..4)
	var lead_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	lead_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.84, 0.86, 0.90, 1.0)
	lead_mat.metallic = 0.90
	lead_mat.roughness = 0.20

	var ic_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	ic_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.10, 0.11, 0.13, 1.0)
	ic_mat.roughness = 0.40

	var res_tan_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	res_tan_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.86, 0.68, 0.42, 1.0)
	var res_blue_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	res_blue_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.22, 0.52, 0.82, 1.0)
	var cap_blue_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	cap_blue_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.10, 0.25, 0.58, 1.0)
	var cap_orange_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	cap_orange_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.92, 0.48, 0.12, 1.0)
	var diode_blk_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	diode_blk_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.14, 0.14, 0.16, 1.0)
	var diode_glass_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	diode_glass_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.92, 0.30, 0.14, 1.0)
	var wire_yel_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	wire_yel_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.95, 0.78, 0.14, 1.0)
	var wire_grn_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	wire_grn_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.16, 0.78, 0.36, 1.0)

	var model_groups: Array[Node3D] = []
	for m_i in range(4):
		var mg := Node3D.new()
		mg.name = "CircuitModelGroup_" + str(m_i)
		mg.visible = (m_i == 0)
		root.add_child(mg)
		model_groups.append(mg)

	var _add_res := func(grp: Node3D, pos: Vector3, mat: StandardMaterial3D) -> void:
		var ld := MeshInstance3D.new()
		var lb := BoxMesh.new()
		lb.size = Vector3(0.026, 0.0012, 0.0012)
		ld.mesh = lb
		ld.position = pos + Vector3(0, -0.0005, 0)
		ld.material_override = lead_mat
		grp.add_child(ld)
		var rb := MeshInstance3D.new()
		var rc := CylinderMesh.new()
		rc.top_radius = 0.0021
		rc.bottom_radius = 0.0021
		rc.height = 0.011
		rb.mesh = rc
		rb.rotation.z = PI * 0.5
		rb.position = pos
		rb.material_override = mat
		grp.add_child(rb)

	var _add_cap := func(grp: Node3D, pos: Vector3, r: float, h: float, mat: StandardMaterial3D) -> void:
		var cp := MeshInstance3D.new()
		var cc := CylinderMesh.new()
		cc.top_radius = r
		cc.bottom_radius = r
		cc.height = h
		cp.mesh = cc
		cp.position = pos
		cp.material_override = mat
		grp.add_child(cp)

	var _add_ic := func(grp: Node3D, pos: Vector3, w: float) -> void:
		var icm := MeshInstance3D.new()
		var icb := BoxMesh.new()
		icb.size = Vector3(w, 0.0048, 0.013)
		icm.mesh = icb
		icm.position = pos
		icm.material_override = ic_mat
		grp.add_child(icm)

	# Model 0 (RC_LPF): 1kΩ Resistor + 100nF Film Cap + Jumper Links
	_add_res.call(model_groups[0], Vector3(-0.038, 0.0112, -0.012), res_tan_mat)
	_add_cap.call(model_groups[0], Vector3(0.002, 0.0130, -0.012), 0.0034, 0.009, cap_orange_mat)
	var jmp0 := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var jb0 := BoxMesh.new()
	jb0.size = Vector3(0.036, 0.0014, 0.0014)
	jmp0.mesh = jb0
	jmp0.position = Vector3(0.036, 0.0105, -0.012)
	jmp0.material_override = wire_yel_mat
	model_groups[0].add_child(jmp0)

	# Model 1 (DIODE_RECT): 1N4007 Power Diode + Large Filter Electrolytic Cap + Load Resistor
	var d1_lead := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var d1_lb := BoxMesh.new()
	d1_lb.size = Vector3(0.028, 0.0013, 0.0013)
	d1_lead.mesh = d1_lb
	d1_lead.position = Vector3(-0.038, 0.0105, -0.012)
	d1_lead.material_override = lead_mat
	model_groups[1].add_child(d1_lead)
	var d1_body := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var d1_c := CylinderMesh.new()
	d1_c.top_radius = 0.0024
	d1_c.bottom_radius = 0.0024
	d1_c.height = 0.011
	d1_body.mesh = d1_c
	d1_body.rotation.z = PI * 0.5
	d1_body.position = Vector3(-0.038, 0.0114, -0.012)
	d1_body.material_override = diode_blk_mat
	model_groups[1].add_child(d1_body)
	_add_cap.call(model_groups[1], Vector3(0.004, 0.0155, -0.010), 0.0052, 0.014, cap_blue_mat)
	_add_res.call(model_groups[1], Vector3(0.042, 0.0112, -0.012), res_blue_mat)

	# Model 2 (ASTABLE_555): Dual DIP ICs (NE555 + ÷2 Flip-Flop) + Timing Resistors RA/RB + Timing Cap
	_add_ic.call(model_groups[2], Vector3(-0.022, 0.0106, -0.002), 0.020)
	_add_ic.call(model_groups[2], Vector3(0.020, 0.0106, -0.002), 0.026)
	_add_res.call(model_groups[2], Vector3(-0.050, 0.0112, -0.016), res_tan_mat)
	_add_res.call(model_groups[2], Vector3(-0.016, 0.0112, -0.018), res_blue_mat)
	_add_cap.call(model_groups[2], Vector3(-0.046, 0.0130, 0.004), 0.0032, 0.009, cap_orange_mat)
	var clk_jmp := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var cjb := BoxMesh.new()
	cjb.size = Vector3(0.024, 0.0014, 0.0014)
	clk_jmp.mesh = cjb
	clk_jmp.position = Vector3(0.000, 0.0115, -0.016)
	clk_jmp.material_override = wire_grn_mat
	model_groups[2].add_child(clk_jmp)

	# Model 3 (PROCEDURAL_MIX): Multi-stage RLC + Op-Amp Buffer + Trimmer Potentiometer
	_add_ic.call(model_groups[3], Vector3(-0.004, 0.0106, -0.002), 0.020)
	_add_res.call(model_groups[3], Vector3(-0.044, 0.0112, -0.014), res_blue_mat)
	_add_cap.call(model_groups[3], Vector3(0.024, 0.0138, -0.013), 0.0042, 0.011, cap_blue_mat)
	var pot := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var pot_b := BoxMesh.new()
	pot_b.size = Vector3(0.009, 0.009, 0.009)
	pot.mesh = pot_b
	pot.position = Vector3(0.046, 0.0130, -0.012)
	pot.material_override = res_blue_mat
	model_groups[3].add_child(pot)
	var d_mix := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var dm_c := CylinderMesh.new()
	dm_c.top_radius = 0.0018
	dm_c.bottom_radius = 0.0018
	dm_c.height = 0.008
	d_mix.mesh = dm_c
	d_mix.rotation.z = PI * 0.5
	d_mix.position = Vector3(-0.044, 0.0110, 0.004)
	d_mix.material_override = diode_glass_mat
	model_groups[3].add_child(d_mix)

	# 4 Upright Gold Test-Point Pins (TP1, TP2, TP3, TP4 GND) with high-contrast label plaques
	var tp_specs := [
		{"key": "BB_TP1", "label": "TP1", "x": -0.056, "col": Color(1.0, 0.82, 0.18)},
		{"key": "BB_TP2", "label": "TP2", "x": -0.020, "col": Color(0.20, 0.96, 0.74)},
		{"key": "BB_TP3", "label": "TP3", "x": 0.018, "col": Color(0.32, 0.82, 1.0)},
		{"key": "BB_TP4", "label": "TP4 GND", "x": 0.056, "col": Color(0.30, 0.32, 0.36)}
	]

	for tp in tp_specs:
		var pin_root := Node3D.new()
		pin_root.position = Vector3(tp["x"], 0.008, 0.018)
		root.add_child(pin_root)

		var bead := MeshInstance3D.new()
		var b_cyl := CylinderMesh.new()
		b_cyl.top_radius = 0.0028
		b_cyl.bottom_radius = 0.0028
		b_cyl.height = 0.0055
		bead.mesh = b_cyl
		bead.position = Vector3(0, 0.0027, 0)
		var b_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
		b_mat.albedo_color = tp["col"]
		bead.material_override = b_mat
		pin_root.add_child(bead)

		var post := MeshInstance3D.new()
		var p_cyl := CylinderMesh.new()
		p_cyl.top_radius = 0.0010
		p_cyl.bottom_radius = 0.0010
		p_cyl.height = 0.016
		post.mesh = p_cyl
		post.position = Vector3(0, 0.010, 0)
		var p_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
		p_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.96, 0.82, 0.32, 1.0)
		p_mat.metallic = 0.88
		p_mat.roughness = 0.20
		post.material_override = p_mat
		pin_root.add_child(post)

		# High-contrast dark silkscreen badge under label
		var badge := MeshInstance3D.new()
		var bg_box := BoxMesh.new()
		bg_box.size = Vector3(0.024, 0.0008, 0.009)
		badge.mesh = bg_box
		badge.position = Vector3(0, 0.0005, 0.0115)
		var bg_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
		bg_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.08, 0.10, 0.13, 1.0)
		badge.material_override = bg_mat
		pin_root.add_child(badge)

		var lbl := Label3D.new()
		lbl.text = tp["label"]
		lbl.pixel_size = 0.00028
		lbl.font_size = 18
		lbl.modulate = tp["col"]
		lbl.outline_size = 2
		lbl.outline_modulate = Color(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0)
		lbl.rotation.x = -PI * 0.5
		lbl.position = Vector3(0, 0.0012, 0.0115)
		pin_root.add_child(lbl)

		snap_targets[tp["key"]] = {
			"node": pin_root,
			"kind": "tip",
			"offset": Vector3(0.0, 0.018, 0.002),
			"vertical": true,
			"label": "Board " + tp["label"]
		}

	# External Bench PSU Power Input Terminals (VCC + Red & GND - Black) on right edge of board
	var pwr_vcc_collar := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var pvc_cyl := CylinderMesh.new()
	pvc_cyl.top_radius = 0.0038
	pvc_cyl.bottom_radius = 0.0038
	pvc_cyl.height = 0.006
	pwr_vcc_collar.mesh = pvc_cyl
	pwr_vcc_collar.position = Vector3(0.072, 0.011, -0.025)
	var pvc_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	pvc_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.90, 0.16, 0.16, 1.0)
	pwr_vcc_collar.material_override = pvc_mat
	root.add_child(pwr_vcc_collar)

	var pwr_gnd_collar := MeshInstance3D.new()
	pwr_gnd_collar.mesh = pvc_cyl
	pwr_gnd_collar.position = Vector3(0.072, 0.011, 0.025)
	var pvg_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	pvg_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.12, 0.13, 0.15, 1.0)
	pwr_gnd_collar.material_override = pvg_mat
	root.add_child(pwr_gnd_collar)

	return {
		"root": root,
		"body_mat": body_mat,
		"bb_details": bb_details,
		"pcb_details": pcb_details,
		"model_groups": model_groups
	}

static func set_active_model(bb_data: Dictionary, model_idx: int) -> void:
	if bb_data.is_empty() or not bb_data.has("model_groups"):
		return
	var groups: Array = bb_data["model_groups"]
	for idx in range(groups.size()):
		(groups[idx] as Node3D).visible = (idx == model_idx)

static func set_board_style(bb_data: Dictionary, is_pcb: bool) -> void:
	if bb_data.is_empty():
		return
	var body_mat: StandardMaterial3D = bb_data["body_mat"]
	var bb_details: Node3D = bb_data["bb_details"]
	var pcb_details: Node3D = bb_data["pcb_details"]
	if is_pcb:
		body_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.05, 0.34, 0.16, 1.0) # Classic FR-4 Solder Mask Green
		body_mat.roughness = 0.35
		bb_details.visible = false
		pcb_details.visible = true
	else:
		body_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.95, 0.94, 0.90, 1.0) # High-contrast Cream Breadboard
		body_mat.roughness = 0.48
		bb_details.visible = true
		pcb_details.visible = false

static func generate_breadboard_signal(model_cfg: Dictionary, tp_key: String, sample_count: int, time_span: float, sim_time_offset: float) -> Dictionary:
	var times := PackedFloat64Array()
	var volts := PackedFloat64Array()
	times.resize(sample_count)
	volts.resize(sample_count)
	var dt := time_span / float(maxi(sample_count - 1, 1))
	var model_id := str(model_cfg.get("id", "RC_LPF"))
	var psu_vcc := float(model_cfg.get("psu_vcc", 12.0))
	var rail_lim := maxf(psu_vcc * 0.45, 0.0)
	var pwr_factor := clampf(psu_vcc / 5.0, 0.0, 1.0)

	for i in range(sample_count):
		var t := sim_time_offset + float(i) * dt
		times[i] = t
		var v := 0.0

		# Bench PSU (GW Instek GPS-3030D) supplies board power: 0.00 V or POWER OFF = 0V across all test points
		if tp_key == "BB_TP4" or psu_vcc <= 0.01:
			v = 0.0
		elif model_id == "RC_LPF" or model_id == "PROCEDURAL_MIX":
			var r_ohm := float(model_cfg.get("r_ohm", 1000.0))
			var c_f := float(model_cfg.get("c_farad", 100e-9))
			var base_amp := float(model_cfg.get("v_in_amp", 3.0))
			var v_amp := base_amp * clampf(psu_vcc / 12.0, 0.0, 1.0)
			var freq := float(model_cfg.get("freq_hz", 1600.0))
			var omega := TAU * freq
			var rc := maxf(r_ohm * c_f, 1e-9)
			var gain := 1.0 / sqrt(1.0 + pow(omega * rc, 2.0))
			var phase := -atan(omega * rc)
			if tp_key == "BB_TP1":
				v = v_amp * sin(omega * t)
			elif tp_key == "BB_TP2":
				v = v_amp * gain * sin(omega * t + phase)
			elif tp_key == "BB_TP3":
				v = v_amp * sin(omega * t) - v_amp * gain * sin(omega * t + phase)
		elif model_id == "DIODE_RECT":
			var v_amp := psu_vcc * 0.25
			var freq := float(model_cfg.get("freq_hz", 500.0))
			var ripple := minf(float(model_cfg.get("ripple_v", 0.45)), v_amp * 0.20)
			var v_ac := v_amp * sin(TAU * freq * t)
			var v_rect := maxf(v_ac - 0.65, 0.0)
			if tp_key == "BB_TP1":
				v = v_ac
			elif tp_key == "BB_TP2":
				v = v_rect
			elif tp_key == "BB_TP3":
				var period := 1.0 / maxf(freq, 1.0)
				var cycle_pos := fmod(t + period * 0.75, period) / period
				var v_peak := maxf(v_amp - 0.65, 0.0)
				v = maxf(v_peak - ripple * cycle_pos, v_rect)
		elif model_id == "ASTABLE_555":
			var vcc := clampf(psu_vcc, 0.0, 18.0) if psu_vcc >= 4.5 else 0.0
			var freq := float(model_cfg.get("freq_hz", 1000.0))
			var phase := fmod(t * freq, 1.0)
			if phase < 0.0:
				phase += 1.0
			if tp_key == "BB_TP1":
				if phase < 0.60:
					v = lerpf(vcc / 3.0, 2.0 * vcc / 3.0, phase / 0.60)
				else:
					v = lerpf(2.0 * vcc / 3.0, vcc / 3.0, (phase - 0.60) / 0.40)
			elif tp_key == "BB_TP2":
				v = vcc if phase < 0.60 else 0.0
			elif tp_key == "BB_TP3":
				var div2_phase := fmod(t * (freq * 0.5), 1.0)
				if div2_phase < 0.0:
					div2_phase += 1.0
				v = vcc if div2_phase < 0.50 else 0.0

		volts[i] = v

	return {"times": times, "voltages": volts, "dt": dt}
