FILE: scripts/visualization/campus_student_builder.gd
========================================
class_name CampusStudentBuilder
extends RefCounted

static var _chest_badge_tex: Texture2D = null

static func build_student(parent: Node3D, variant: String = "man", is_seated: bool = true) -> Dictionary:
	var av := Node3D.new()
	parent.add_child(av)

	var shirt_blue := Color(0.16, 0.30, 0.47)
	var shirt_dark := Color(0.11, 0.21, 0.34)
	var band_yellow := Color(0.92, 0.80, 0.22)
	var pants_black := Color(0.07, 0.08, 0.10)
	var shoe_black := Color(0.05, 0.05, 0.06)
	var skin_col := Color(0.84, 0.65, 0.53)
	var hair_black := Color(0.08, 0.08, 0.10)
	var hijab_black := Color(0.07, 0.07, 0.09)

	var is_female := (variant == "hijab" or variant == "woman")
	var hip_y := 0.47 if is_seated else 0.90

	# 1. Pelvis & Articulated Legs
	_add_loft(av, [
		[-0.065, 0.135, 0.092],
		[0.0,    0.155, 0.108],
		[0.065,  0.145, 0.098]
	], Vector3(0, hip_y, 0), pants_black, 0.72, 10)

	var hip_x := 0.085 if is_female else 0.094
	var leg_l := _build_leg(av, Vector3(-hip_x, hip_y - 0.04, 0.0), pants_black, shoe_black, is_seated)
	var leg_r := _build_leg(av, Vector3(hip_x, hip_y - 0.04, 0.0), pants_black, shoe_black, is_seated)

	if is_seated:
		_add_cyl(av, 0.18, 0.032, Vector3(0, 0.44, 0.03), Color(0.16, 0.18, 0.21), 0.50, 14)
		_add_cyl(av, 0.022, 0.44, Vector3(0, 0.22, 0.03), Color(0.10, 0.11, 0.13), 0.40, 10)

	# 2. Tailored DTE Work Shirt Torso + Collar + Pockets + DTE/PENS Crest
	var torso := Node3D.new()
	torso.position = Vector3(0, hip_y + 0.05, 0)
	av.add_child(torso)

	var w_bot := 0.142 if is_female else 0.162
	var w_mid := 0.132 if is_female else 0.158
	var w_top := 0.182 if is_female else 0.208
	_add_loft(torso, [
		[0.0,  w_bot, 0.102],
		[0.14, w_mid, 0.096],
		[0.32, w_top, 0.118],
		[0.43, w_top * 0.92, 0.105],
		[0.46, 0.085, 0.078]
	], Vector3.ZERO, shirt_blue, 0.62, 12)

	_add_loft(torso, [
		[0.445, 0.084, 0.080],
		[0.478, 0.074, 0.072]
	], Vector3.ZERO, shirt_dark, 0.58, 12)

	_add_box(torso, Vector3(0.022, 0.38, 0.008), Vector3(0.0, 0.22, 0.114), shirt_dark, 0.60)
	for by in [0.12, 0.21, 0.30, 0.39]:
		_add_cyl(torso, 0.0042, 0.005, Vector3(0.0, by, 0.119), Color(0.06, 0.07, 0.09), 0.35, 8).rotation.x = PI * 0.5

	for px in [-0.078, 0.078]:
		_add_box(torso, Vector3(0.068, 0.078, 0.012), Vector3(px, 0.275, 0.114), shirt_dark, 0.62)
		_add_box(torso, Vector3(0.072, 0.022, 0.015), Vector3(px, 0.306, 0.116), shirt_blue, 0.58)
	_add_box(torso, Vector3(0.008, 0.090, 0.008), Vector3(0.025, 0.275, 0.117), Color(0.05, 0.05, 0.07), 0.40)

	var badge_plate := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var qm := QuadMesh.new()
	qm.size = Vector2(0.255, 0.070)
	badge_plate.mesh = qm
	badge_plate.position = Vector3(0.0, 0.362, 0.116)
	badge_plate.rotation.x = -0.08
	var bmat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	bmat.albedo_texture = _get_chest_badge_texture()
	bmat.transparency = BaseMaterial3D.TRANSPARENCY_ALPHA
	bmat.roughness = 0.55
	badge_plate.material_override = bmat
	torso.add_child(badge_plate)

	# 3. Neck & Properly Proportioned Low-Poly Human Head (Open Face, No Helmet Overlap)
	var head_pivot := Node3D.new()
	head_pivot.position = Vector3(0.0, 0.465, 0.0)
	torso.add_child(head_pivot)

	# Slender human neck
	_add_cyl(head_pivot, 0.038, 0.082, Vector3(0, 0.038, 0), skin_col, 0.58, 12)

	# Anatomically proportioned human head (height 0.205m, width 0.152m, depth 0.176m)
	var jaw_rx := 0.044 if is_female else 0.050
	var cheek_rx := 0.072 if is_female else 0.077
	var head_node := _add_loft(head_pivot, [
		[0.052, 0.020, 0.026],   # Chin
		[0.080, jaw_rx, 0.062],  # Jawline
		[0.122, cheek_rx, 0.084],# Cheekbones
		[0.162, cheek_rx * 1.02, 0.088], # Brow / temple
		[0.202, 0.064, 0.076],   # Upper forehead / cranium
		[0.224, 0.022, 0.028]    # Crown
	], Vector3(0, 0, 0.012), skin_col, 0.56, 14)

	# Crisp Facial Features positioned cleanly on the front face (+Z = +0.088)
	for ex in [-0.027, 0.027]:
		_add_box(head_node, Vector3(0.022, 0.005, 0.006), Vector3(ex, 0.160, 0.085), hair_black, 0.7)
		_add_box(head_node, Vector3(0.018, 0.009, 0.005), Vector3(ex, 0.148, 0.085), Color(0.95, 0.95, 0.94), 0.25)
		_add_box(head_node, Vector3(0.009, 0.008, 0.007), Vector3(ex, 0.148, 0.086), Color(0.12, 0.09, 0.07), 0.20)
	# Sculpted nose bridge & tip
	var nose := _add_loft(head_node, [
		[0.116, 0.008, 0.013],
		[0.150, 0.005, 0.007]
	], Vector3(0.0, 0.0, 0.088), skin_col.darkened(0.05), 0.55, 6)
	nose.rotation.x = -0.18
	# Subtle lips
	_add_box(head_node, Vector3(0.022, 0.004, 0.006), Vector3(0.0, 0.094, 0.078), Color(0.62, 0.39, 0.36), 0.55)

	if variant == "hijab":
		# Hijab Hood shifted BACKWARD (z = -0.022) so the entire face (z > +0.045) remains completely visible!
		_add_loft(head_pivot, [
			[0.048, 0.078, 0.074],   # Under-chin scarf wrap
			[0.095, 0.084, 0.082],   # Side cheek framing
			[0.155, 0.088, 0.088],   # Upper hood framing forehead
			[0.205, 0.080, 0.084],   # Crown hood
			[0.232, 0.026, 0.030]    # Top crown
		], Vector3(0.0, 0.0, -0.022), hijab_black, 0.76, 14)
		# Draped neck & chest hijab fold
		_add_loft(head_pivot, [
			[-0.015, 0.088, 0.082],
			[0.035,  0.076, 0.074],
			[0.062,  0.068, 0.068]
		], Vector3(0.0, 0.0, -0.006), hijab_black, 0.78, 12)
		# Back draped triangular veil over upper back
		var back_veil := _add_loft(torso, [
			[0.30, 0.022, 0.014],
			[0.38, 0.088, 0.034],
			[0.45, 0.105, 0.048]
		], Vector3(0.0, 0.0, -0.095), hijab_black, 0.76, 10)
		back_veil.rotation.x = 0.12
	elif variant == "woman":
		# Shoulder-Length Parted Hair shifted BACKWARD (z = -0.026) framing sides & back of head
		_add_loft(head_pivot, [
			[-0.010, 0.094, 0.076],  # Shoulder-length hair hem
			[0.068,  0.090, 0.080],  # Neck curtain
			[0.145,  0.088, 0.086],  # Cheek framing hair
			[0.198,  0.080, 0.084],  # Upper crown hair
			[0.232,  0.032, 0.036]
		], Vector3(0.0, 0.0, -0.026), hair_black, 0.65, 14)
	else:
		# Cropped Men's Hair Cap shifted BACKWARD (z = -0.016) + Visible Ears
		_add_loft(head_pivot, [
			[0.115, 0.078, 0.078],
			[0.172, 0.082, 0.086],
			[0.208, 0.070, 0.078],
			[0.230, 0.028, 0.034]
		], Vector3(0.0, 0.0, -0.016), hair_black, 0.65, 14)
		for ear_x in [-0.076, 0.076]:
			_add_cyl(head_node, 0.012, 0.028, Vector3(ear_x, 0.140, -0.004), skin_col, 0.58, 8)

	# 4. Articulated Tapered Arms + Yellow Sleeve Bands + Hands
	var sh_x := 0.198 if is_female else 0.224
	var arm_l := _build_arm(torso, Vector3(-sh_x, 0.40, 0.0), shirt_blue, shirt_dark, band_yellow, skin_col, true)
	var arm_r := _build_arm(torso, Vector3(sh_x, 0.40, 0.0), shirt_blue, shirt_dark, band_yellow, skin_col, false)

	if is_seated:
		torso.rotation.x = 0.16
		arm_l["shoulder"].rotation = Vector3(-0.78, 0.18, 0.10)
		arm_l["elbow"].rotation.x = -0.65
		arm_r["shoulder"].rotation = Vector3(-0.84, -0.22, -0.10)
		arm_r["elbow"].rotation.x = -0.72
	else:
		arm_l["shoulder"].rotation.z = 0.12
		arm_r["shoulder"].rotation.z = -0.12

	return {
		"root": av,
		"torso": torso,
		"head": head_pivot,
		"arm_l": arm_l,
		"arm_r": arm_r,
		"leg_l": leg_l,
		"leg_r": leg_r
	}

static func _build_arm(parent: Node3D, pos: Vector3, shirt_col: Color, pocket_col: Color, yellow_col: Color, skin_col: Color, is_left: bool) -> Dictionary:
	var shoulder := Node3D.new()
	shoulder.position = pos
	parent.add_child(shoulder)

	_add_loft(shoulder, [
		[-0.23, 0.042, 0.042],
		[-0.12, 0.048, 0.048],
		[0.01,  0.054, 0.054]
	], Vector3.ZERO, shirt_col, 0.62, 10)
	_add_loft(shoulder, [
		[-0.145, 0.0485, 0.0485],
		[-0.102, 0.0505, 0.0505]
	], Vector3.ZERO, yellow_col, 0.48, 10)
	var side_x := -0.046 if is_left else 0.046
	_add_box(shoulder, Vector3(0.010, 0.064, 0.044), Vector3(side_x, -0.172, 0.0), pocket_col, 0.62)

	var elbow := Node3D.new()
	elbow.position = Vector3(0, -0.23, 0)
	shoulder.add_child(elbow)
	_add_loft(elbow, [
		[-0.22, 0.032, 0.032],
		[-0.11, 0.038, 0.038],
		[0.0,   0.042, 0.042]
	], Vector3.ZERO, shirt_col, 0.62, 10)
	_add_loft(elbow, [
		[-0.30, 0.016, 0.022],
		[-0.25, 0.020, 0.030],
		[-0.22, 0.024, 0.026]
	], Vector3.ZERO, skin_col, 0.55, 8)

	return {"shoulder": shoulder, "elbow": elbow}

static func _build_leg(parent: Node3D, pos: Vector3, pants_col: Color, shoe_col: Color, is_seated: bool) -> Dictionary:
	var hip := Node3D.new()
	hip.position = pos
	parent.add_child(hip)

	_add_loft(hip, [
		[-0.42, 0.058, 0.062],
		[-0.21, 0.068, 0.074],
		[0.0,   0.076, 0.082]
	], Vector3.ZERO, pants_col, 0.72, 10)

	var knee := Node3D.new()
	knee.position = Vector3(0, -0.42, 0)
	hip.add_child(knee)
	_add_loft(knee, [
		[-0.41, 0.048, 0.052],
		[-0.20, 0.054, 0.058],
		[0.0,   0.058, 0.062]
	], Vector3.ZERO, pants_col, 0.72, 10)
	var shoe := _add_loft(knee, [
		[-0.095, 0.032, 0.022],
		[-0.025, 0.042, 0.028],
		[0.065,  0.044, 0.030]
	], Vector3(0, -0.425, 0.035), shoe_col, 0.45, 8)
	shoe.rotation.x = PI * 0.5

	if is_seated:
		hip.rotation.x = -1.48
		knee.rotation.x = 1.48

	return {"hip": hip, "knee": knee}

static func _get_chest_badge_texture() -> Texture2D:
	if _chest_badge_tex != null:
		return _chest_badge_tex

	var w := 512
	var h := 144
	var img := Image.create(w, h, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
	img.fill(Color(0, 0, 0, 0))

	var white := Color(0.97, 0.98, 1.0, 0.96)
	var cyan_wing := Color(0.78, 0.90, 1.0, 0.96)

	_draw_dte_text(img, 44, 38, 6, white)

	var cx := 392
	var cy := 50
	for wy in range(-10, 12):
		var span: int = 46 - absi(wy) * 2
		for wx in range(-span, span + 1):
			if absi(wx) > 16:
				img.set_pixel(cx + wx, cy + wy, cyan_wing)
	for dy in range(-22, 23):
		for dx in range(-22, 23):
			var r2 := dx * dx + dy * dy
			if r2 <= 440 and r2 >= 190:
				img.set_pixel(cx + dx, cy + dy, white)
			elif r2 < 120 and dy < 4:
				img.set_pixel(cx + dx, cy + dy - 4, white)

	_draw_pens_text(img, 344, 84, 4, white)

	_chest_badge_tex = ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)
	return _chest_badge_tex

static func _draw_rect_px(img: Image, x0: int, y0: int, rw: int, rh: int, col: Color) -> void:
	for y in range(maxi(0, y0), mini(img.get_height(), y0 + rh)):
		for x in range(maxi(0, x0), mini(img.get_width(), x0 + rw)):
			img.set_pixel(x, y, col)

static func _draw_dte_text(img: Image, x: int, y: int, s: int, col: Color) -> void:
	_draw_rect_px(img, x, y, s, s * 8, col)
	_draw_rect_px(img, x + s, y, s * 3, s, col)
	_draw_rect_px(img, x + s, y + s * 7, s * 3, s, col)
	_draw_rect_px(img, x + s * 4, y + s, s, s * 6, col)
	var tx := x + s * 7
	_draw_rect_px(img, tx, y, s * 5, s, col)
	_draw_rect_px(img, tx + s * 2, y + s, s, s * 7, col)
	var ex := x + s * 14
	_draw_rect_px(img, ex, y, s, s * 8, col)
	_draw_rect_px(img, ex + s, y, s * 4, s, col)
	_draw_rect_px(img, ex + s, y + s * 3, s * 3, s, col)
	_draw_rect_px(img, ex + s, y + s * 7, s * 4, s, col)

static func _draw_pens_text(img: Image, x: int, y: int, s: int, col: Color) -> void:
	_draw_rect_px(img, x, y, s, s * 7, col)
	_draw_rect_px(img, x + s, y, s * 3, s, col)
	_draw_rect_px(img, x + s, y + s * 3, s * 3, s, col)
	_draw_rect_px(img, x + s * 4, y + s, s, s * 2, col)
	var ex := x + s * 6
	_draw_rect_px(img, ex, y, s, s * 7, col)
	_draw_rect_px(img, ex + s, y, s * 3, s, col)
	_draw_rect_px(img, ex + s, y + s * 3, s * 2, s, col)
	_draw_rect_px(img, ex + s, y + s * 6, s * 3, s, col)
	var nx := x + s * 11
	_draw_rect_px(img, nx, y, s, s * 7, col)
	_draw_rect_px(img, nx + s, y + s, s, s * 2, col)
	_draw_rect_px(img, nx + s * 2, y + s * 3, s, s * 2, col)
	_draw_rect_px(img, nx + s * 3, y + s * 5, s, s, col)
	_draw_rect_px(img, nx + s * 4, y, s, s * 7, col)
	var sx := x + s * 16
	_draw_rect_px(img, sx + s, y, s * 3, s, col)
	_draw_rect_px(img, sx, y + s, s, s * 2, col)
	_draw_rect_px(img, sx + s, y + s * 3, s * 2, s, col)
	_draw_rect_px(img, sx + s * 3, y + s * 4, s, s * 2, col)
	_draw_rect_px(img, sx, y + s * 6, s * 3, s, col)

static func _add_loft(parent: Node3D, rings: Array, pos: Vector3, col: Color, rough := 0.6, segs := 12) -> MeshInstance3D:
	var st := SurfaceTool.new()
	st.begin(Mesh.PRIMITIVE_TRIANGLES)
	var ring_verts: Array = []
	for r in rings:
		var y := float(r[0])
		var rx := float(r[1])
		var rz := float(r[2])
		var pts: Array[Vector3] = []
		for i in range(segs):
			var ang := TAU * float(i) / float(segs)
			pts.append(Vector3(cos(ang) * rx, y, sin(ang) * rz))
		ring_verts.append(pts)

	for ri in range(ring_verts.size() - 1):
		var r0: Array = ring_verts[ri]
		var r1: Array = ring_verts[ri + 1]
		for i in range(segs):
			var ni := (i + 1) % segs
			var p00: Vector3 = r0[i]
			var p10: Vector3 = r0[ni]
			var p11: Vector3 = r1[ni]
			var p01: Vector3 = r1[i]
			var n1 := (p10 - p00).cross(p11 - p00).normalized()
			st.set_normal(n1); st.add_vertex(p00); st.add_vertex(p10); st.add_vertex(p11)
			var n2 := (p11 - p00).cross(p01 - p00).normalized()
			st.set_normal(n2); st.add_vertex(p00); st.add_vertex(p11); st.add_vertex(p01)

	var bot: Array = ring_verts[0]
	var cy_b := float(rings[0][0])
	for i in range(segs):
		var ni := (i + 1) % segs
		st.set_normal(Vector3.DOWN)
		st.add_vertex(Vector3(0, cy_b, 0)); st.add_vertex(bot[ni]); st.add_vertex(bot[i])

	var top: Array = ring_verts[ring_verts.size() - 1]
	var cy_t := float(rings[rings.size() - 1][0])
	for i in range(segs):
		var ni := (i + 1) % segs
		st.set_normal(Vector3.UP)
		st.add_vertex(Vector3(0, cy_t, 0)); st.add_vertex(top[i]); st.add_vertex(top[ni])

	var mi := MeshInstance3D.new()
	mi.mesh = st.commit()
	mi.position = pos
	var mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	mat.albedo_color = col
	mat.roughness = rough
	mi.material_override = mat
	parent.add_child(mi)
	return mi

static func _add_box(parent: Node3D, sz: Vector3, pos: Vector3, col: Color, rough := 0.5) -> MeshInstance3D:
	var mi := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var bm := BoxMesh.new()
	bm.size = sz
	mi.mesh = bm
	mi.position = pos
	var mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	mat.albedo_color = col
	mat.roughness = rough
	mi.material_override = mat
	parent.add_child(mi)
	return mi

static func _add_cyl(parent: Node3D, rad: float, h: float, pos: Vector3, col: Color, rough := 0.5, segs := 12) -> MeshInstance3D:
	var mi := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var cm := CylinderMesh.new()
	cm.top_radius = rad
	cm.bottom_radius = rad
	cm.height = h
	cm.radial_segments = segs
	mi.mesh = cm
	mi.position = pos
	var mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	mat.albedo_color = col
	mat.roughness = rough
	mi.material_override = mat
	parent.add_child(mi)
	return mi

static func build_studio_workbench(parent: Node) -> Dictionary:
	var env_root := Node3D.new()
	env_root.name = "StudioElectronicsLab"
	parent.add_child(env_root)

	# 1. Primary Workbench Desk + Heavy Frame Legs + Campus Under-Apron AC Outlet Block
	var desk := _add_box(env_root, Vector3(1.85, 0.04, 1.12), Vector3(0.02, -0.02, 0.14), Color(0.86, 0.85, 0.82), 0.55)
	for lx in [-0.82, 0.86]:
		for lz in [-0.34, 0.62]:
			_add_box(env_root, Vector3(0.05, 0.72, 0.05), Vector3(lx, -0.38, lz), Color(0.14, 0.11, 0.09), 0.45)

	var outlet_box := _add_box(env_root, Vector3(0.16, 0.042, 0.032), Vector3(-0.34, -0.062, 0.685), Color(0.86, 0.85, 0.81), 0.4)
	for ox in [-0.052, -0.018, 0.018, 0.052]:
		_add_cyl(outlet_box, 0.011, 0.006, Vector3(ox, 0.0, 0.016), Color(0.16, 0.16, 0.18), 0.4).rotation.x = PI * 0.5

	# 2. ESD Anti-Static Mat + Grounding Snap Puck + Precision Stylus
	var esd := _add_box(env_root, Vector3(1.12, 0.003, 0.68), Vector3(0.02, 0.0015, 0.18), Color(0.07, 0.38, 0.68), 0.60)
	_add_box(env_root, Vector3(0.032, 0.010, 0.038), Vector3(0.45, 0.007, 0.44), Color(0.11, 0.12, 0.14), 0.45)
	_add_cyl(env_root, 0.004, 0.004, Vector3(0.45, 0.013, 0.44), Color(0.85, 0.72, 0.22), 0.25, 0.85)

	# =========================================================================
	# STUDIO PROPS GROUP
	# =========================================================================
	var studio_group := Node3D.new()
	studio_group.name = "StudioRoomProps"
	env_root.add_child(studio_group)

	var wall := _add_box(studio_group, Vector3(2.4, 1.35, 0.04), Vector3(0.02, 0.58, -0.26), Color(0.88, 0.87, 0.84), 0.85)
	_add_box(studio_group, Vector3(0.04, 0.48, 0.03), Vector3(-0.34, 0.24, -0.22), Color(0.10, 0.11, 0.13), 0.4)
	_add_box(studio_group, Vector3(0.58, 0.34, 0.024), Vector3(-0.16, 0.43, -0.20), Color(0.09, 0.10, 0.12), 0.35)
	var mon_scr := _add_box(studio_group, Vector3(0.55, 0.31, 0.003), Vector3(-0.16, 0.43, -0.187), Color(0.04, 0.06, 0.09), 0.15)
	var scr_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	scr_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.04, 0.06, 0.09)
	scr_mat.emission_enabled = true
	scr_mat.emission = Color(0.05, 0.14, 0.22)
	scr_mat.emission_energy_multiplier = 0.75
	mon_scr.material_override = scr_mat

	var cable_colors := [
		Color(0.85, 0.20, 0.18), Color(0.18, 0.65, 0.32), Color(0.92, 0.76, 0.16),
		Color(0.18, 0.45, 0.85), Color(0.15, 0.16, 0.18), Color(0.88, 0.42, 0.14)
	]
	for i in range(10):
		var cx := -0.34 + float(i) * 0.036
		_add_cyl(studio_group, 0.0024, 0.26, Vector3(cx, 0.14, -0.215), cable_colors[i % cable_colors.size()], 0.5)

	# Articulated Overhead Desk Task Lamp
	var lamp_arm := Node3D.new()
	lamp_arm.position = Vector3(-0.56, 0.0, -0.10)
	studio_group.add_child(lamp_arm)

	var p_base := Vector3(0.0, 0.016, 0.0)
	var p_elbow := Vector3(0.05, 0.30, 0.05)
	var p_wrist := Vector3(0.46, 0.44, 0.24)
	var p_head := Vector3(0.46, 0.415, 0.24)

	_add_cyl(lamp_arm, 0.044, 0.016, Vector3(0.0, 0.008, 0.0), Color(0.12, 0.13, 0.15), 0.4, 0.65)
	_add_cyl(lamp_arm, 0.014, 0.024, Vector3(0.0, 0.020, 0.0), Color(0.18, 0.19, 0.22), 0.35, 0.75)
	_add_strut(lamp_arm, p_base, p_elbow, 0.0085, Color(0.15, 0.16, 0.18))
	_add_joint_sphere(lamp_arm, p_elbow, 0.013, Color(0.22, 0.24, 0.28))
	_add_strut(lamp_arm, p_elbow, p_wrist, 0.0075, Color(0.15, 0.16, 0.18))
	_add_joint_sphere(lamp_arm, p_wrist, 0.012, Color(0.22, 0.24, 0.28))
	_add_strut(lamp_arm, p_wrist, p_head, 0.0070, Color(0.18, 0.19, 0.22))

	var shade := _add_box(lamp_arm, Vector3(0.28, 0.018, 0.050), p_head + Vector3(0.0, -0.006, 0.0), Color(0.12, 0.13, 0.15), 0.35)
	shade.rotation.y = -0.14
	var bulb_bar := _add_box(lamp_arm, Vector3(0.25, 0.005, 0.034), p_head + Vector3(0.0, -0.016, 0.0), Color(1.0, 0.85, 0.60), 0.2)
	bulb_bar.rotation.y = -0.14

	bulb_mat.albedo_color = Color(1.0, 0.88, 0.68)
	bulb_mat.emission_enabled = true
	bulb_mat.emission = Color(1.0, 0.80, 0.52)
	bulb_mat.emission_energy_multiplier = 0.0
	bulb_bar.material_override = bulb_mat

	var task_light := OmniLight3D.new()
	task_light.position = Vector3(-0.10, 0.38, 0.14)
	task_light.omni_range = 1.65
	task_light.omni_attenuation = 1.15
	task_light.light_color = Color(1.0, 0.80, 0.52)
	task_light.light_energy = 0.0
	task_light.shadow_enabled = false
	studio_group.add_child(task_light)

	var hanger := _add_box(studio_group, Vector3(0.18, 0.012, 0.045), Vector3(0.68, 0.48, -0.22), Color(0.14, 0.15, 0.18), 0.4)
	hanger.rotation.y = -0.25
	for k in range(8):
		var lx := 0.61 + float(k) * 0.020
		var l_len := 0.34 + float(k % 3) * 0.05
		_add_cyl(studio_group, 0.0020, l_len, Vector3(lx, 0.48 - l_len * 0.5, -0.205), cable_colors[k % cable_colors.size()], 0.45)

	# =========================================================================
	# CAMPUS ELECTRONICS LAB ROOM (Back-Row View at 4:00 PM Golden Afternoon)
	# =========================================================================
	var campus_group := Node3D.new()
	campus_group.name = "CampusElectronicsLabRoom"
	campus_group.visible = false
	env_root.add_child(campus_group)
	var campus_anim := _build_campus_lab_room(campus_group)

	# =========================================================================
	# SHARED BENCH INSTRUMENTS (Present in both Studio & Campus Lab Modes)
	# =========================================================================
	var hdmm := Node3D.new()
	hdmm.position = Vector3(-0.47, 0.019, 0.31)
	hdmm.rotation = Vector3(0.0, 0.48, 0.0)
	env_root.add_child(hdmm)
	_add_box(hdmm, Vector3(0.086, 0.032, 0.165), Vector3(0, 0, 0), Color(0.95, 0.74, 0.10), 0.55)
	_add_box(hdmm, Vector3(0.072, 0.034, 0.150), Vector3(0, 0.001, 0), Color(0.16, 0.17, 0.20), 0.45)
	var hdmm_lcd := _add_box(hdmm, Vector3(0.056, 0.003, 0.036), Vector3(0, 0.0185, -0.044), Color(0.08, 0.22, 0.18), 0.2)
	var hlcd_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	hlcd_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.08, 0.22, 0.18)
	hlcd_mat.emission_enabled = true
	hlcd_mat.emission = Color(0.18, 0.75, 0.58)
	hlcd_mat.emission_energy_multiplier = 0.85
	hdmm_lcd.material_override = hlcd_mat
	for bx in [-0.018, -0.006, 0.006, 0.018]:
		_add_box(hdmm, Vector3(0.008, 0.004, 0.006), Vector3(bx, 0.019, -0.018), Color(0.95, 0.76, 0.12), 0.4)
	_add_cyl(hdmm, 0.019, 0.008, Vector3(0, 0.020, 0.010), Color(0.22, 0.24, 0.28), 0.35)
	_add_box(hdmm, Vector3(0.004, 0.010, 0.034), Vector3(0, 0.021, 0.010), Color(0.92, 0.93, 0.95), 0.3)
	var jack_specs := [
		[-0.020, Color(0.85, 0.15, 0.15)],
		[0.0, Color(0.10, 0.11, 0.13)],
		[0.020, Color(0.85, 0.15, 0.15)]
	]
	for js in jack_specs:
		_add_cyl(hdmm, 0.0055, 0.006, Vector3(js[0], 0.019, 0.054), js[1], 0.35)
		_add_cyl(hdmm, 0.0025, 0.007, Vector3(js[0], 0.0195, 0.054), Color(0.82, 0.72, 0.28), 0.25, 0.85)

	var dso_root := Node3D.new()
	dso_root.position = Vector3(0.02, 0.0, 0.01)
	env_root.add_child(dso_root)
	_add_box(dso_root, Vector3(0.24, 0.135, 0.12), Vector3(0.0, 0.0675, 0.0), Color(0.15, 0.16, 0.18), 0.45)
	var dso_lcd := _add_box(dso_root, Vector3(0.135, 0.095, 0.003), Vector3(-0.04, 0.072, 0.061), Color(0.03, 0.07, 0.11), 0.15)
	var dso_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	dso_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.03, 0.07, 0.11)
	dso_mat.emission_enabled = true
	dso_mat.emission = Color(0.06, 0.22, 0.34)
	dso_mat.emission_energy_multiplier = 0.80
	dso_lcd.material_override = dso_mat
	for ky in [0.095, 0.068, 0.042]:
		_add_cyl(dso_root, 0.007, 0.012, Vector3(0.065, ky, 0.064), Color(0.22, 0.24, 0.27), 0.4).rotation.x = PI * 0.5
	for bx in [-0.08, -0.045, -0.01, 0.025]:
		_add_cyl(dso_root, 0.0055, 0.014, Vector3(bx, 0.014, 0.065), Color(0.78, 0.80, 0.83), 0.25, 0.85).rotation.x = PI * 0.5

	var rack_x := 0.46
	var rack_z := -0.08
	var wood_col := Color(0.76, 0.62, 0.44)
	_add_box(env_root, Vector3(0.016, 0.38, 0.24), Vector3(rack_x - 0.145, 0.19, rack_z), wood_col, 0.72)
	_add_box(env_root, Vector3(0.016, 0.38, 0.24), Vector3(rack_x + 0.145, 0.19, rack_z), wood_col, 0.72)
	_add_box(env_root, Vector3(0.306, 0.016, 0.24), Vector3(rack_x, 0.175, rack_z), wood_col, 0.72)
	_add_box(env_root, Vector3(0.306, 0.016, 0.24), Vector3(rack_x, 0.375, rack_z), wood_col, 0.72)

	_add_box(env_root, Vector3(0.25, 0.082, 0.21), Vector3(rack_x, 0.325, rack_z), Color(0.82, 0.81, 0.77), 0.45)
	_add_box(env_root, Vector3(0.236, 0.072, 0.004), Vector3(rack_x, 0.325, rack_z + 0.105), Color(0.22, 0.24, 0.27), 0.4)
	var dmm_disp := _add_box(env_root, Vector3(0.115, 0.028, 0.003), Vector3(rack_x - 0.048, 0.338, rack_z + 0.108), Color(0.04, 0.16, 0.12), 0.2)
	var dmm_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	dmm_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.04, 0.16, 0.12)
	dmm_mat.emission_enabled = true
	dmm_mat.emission = Color(0.12, 0.75, 0.52)
	dmm_mat.emission_energy_multiplier = 0.95
	dmm_disp.material_override = dmm_mat
	for col_b in range(5):
		for row_b in range(2):
			_add_box(env_root, Vector3(0.014, 0.007, 0.004), Vector3(rack_x - 0.088 + float(col_b) * 0.020, 0.312 - float(row_b) * 0.012, rack_z + 0.108), Color(0.75, 0.76, 0.78), 0.4)
	var dmm_jacks := [Color(0.85, 0.15, 0.15), Color(0.12, 0.12, 0.14), Color(0.85, 0.15, 0.15)]
	for idx_j in range(dmm_jacks.size()):
		_add_cyl(env_root, 0.005, 0.011, Vector3(rack_x + 0.095, 0.342 - float(idx_j) * 0.018, rack_z + 0.109), dmm_jacks[idx_j], 0.35).rotation.x = PI * 0.5

	_add_box(env_root, Vector3(0.25, 0.088, 0.21), Vector3(rack_x, 0.232, rack_z), Color(0.22, 0.24, 0.27), 0.45)
	var psu_disp := _add_box(env_root, Vector3(0.105, 0.042, 0.003), Vector3(rack_x - 0.052, 0.238, rack_z + 0.106), Color(0.05, 0.11, 0.20), 0.2)
	var psu_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	psu_mat.albedo_color = Color(0.05, 0.11, 0.20)
	psu_mat.emission_enabled = true
	psu_mat.emission = Color(0.22, 0.62, 1.0)
	psu_mat.emission_energy_multiplier = 0.90
	psu_disp.material_override = psu_mat
	for kx in [0.025, 0.062]:
		_add_cyl(env_root, 0.0085, 0.012, Vector3(rack_x + kx, 0.248, rack_z + 0.109), Color(0.14, 0.15, 0.18), 0.35).rotation.x = PI * 0.5
		_add_cyl(env_root, 0.0065, 0.012, Vector3(rack_x + kx, 0.222, rack_z + 0.109), Color(0.14, 0.15, 0.18), 0.35).rotation.x = PI * 0.5
	for px in [0.020, 0.045, 0.070]:
		var pc := Color(0.85, 0.15, 0.15) if px != 0.045 else Color(0.16, 0.68, 0.32)
		_add_cyl(env_root, 0.0045, 0.011, Vector3(rack_x + px, 0.198, rack_z + 0.109), pc, 0.35).rotation.x = PI * 0.5

	var meter_root := Node3D.new()
	meter_root.position = Vector3(0.48, 0.0, 0.24)
	meter_root.rotation.y = -0.52
	env_root.add_child(meter_root)
	_add_box(meter_root, Vector3(0.115, 0.155, 0.10), Vector3(0.0, 0.0775, 0.0), Color(0.30, 0.32, 0.35), 0.45)
	_add_box(meter_root, Vector3(0.092, 0.068, 0.004), Vector3(0.0, 0.108, 0.051), Color(0.95, 0.94, 0.90), 0.25)
	_add_box(meter_root, Vector3(0.068, 0.003, 0.002), Vector3(0.0, 0.122, 0.0535), Color(0.12, 0.12, 0.14), 0.4)
	var needle := _add_box(meter_root, Vector3(0.002, 0.036, 0.002), Vector3(-0.008, 0.106, 0.0535), Color(0.85, 0.14, 0.14), 0.3)
	needle.rotation.z = 0.32
	for b_x in [-0.026, 0.0, 0.026]:
		var b_c := Color(0.85, 0.15, 0.15) if b_x != 0.0 else Color(0.12, 0.12, 0.14)
		_add_cyl(meter_root, 0.0055, 0.014, Vector3(b_x, 0.032, 0.055), b_c, 0.35).rotation.x = PI * 0.5

	_add_cyl(env_root, 0.018, 0.032, Vector3(0.45, 0.018, 0.32), Color(0.18, 0.42, 0.78), 0.45)
	_add_cyl(env_root, 0.014, 0.022, Vector3(0.45, 0.018, 0.32), Color(0.76, 0.78, 0.80), 0.25, 0.85)

	return {
		"desk_mat": desk.material_override,
		"esd_mat": esd.material_override,
		"wall_mat": wall.material_override,
		"bulb_mat": bulb_mat,
		"task_light": task_light,
		"studio_group": studio_group,
		"campus_group": campus_group,
		"campus_anim": campus_anim
	}

static func _build_campus_lab_room(root: Node3D) -> Dictionary:
	# User's bench is positioned at the back-right row (z=0), looking forward toward the front whiteboard (z=-4.8)
	var floor_y := -0.74
	var wall_cream := Color(0.88, 0.85, 0.77)
	var mahogany := Color(0.27, 0.16, 0.10)
	var tile_dark := Color(0.23, 0.24, 0.26)

	# 1. Charcoal Ceramic Tiled Floor + White PVC Cable Conduits
	_add_box(root, Vector3(9.0, 0.04, 11.5), Vector3(-0.3, floor_y - 0.02, -1.2), tile_dark, 0.32)
	for tz in [-3.2, -1.7, -0.2, 1.3]:
		_add_box(root, Vector3(5.6, 0.008, 0.035), Vector3(-0.5, floor_y + 0.004, tz), Color(0.90, 0.90, 0.88), 0.4)

	# 2. Front Classroom Wall (z = -4.9) with Raised Wooden Lecturer Stage, Whiteboard, Clock & 5R Poster
	_add_box(root, Vector3(9.0, 3.2, 0.12), Vector3(-0.3, floor_y + 1.60, -4.9), wall_cream, 0.85)
	_add_box(root, Vector3(4.8, 0.10, 1.65), Vector3(0.5, floor_y + 0.05, -4.0), Color(0.32, 0.22, 0.14), 0.55)
	_add_box(root, Vector3(2.95, 1.22, 0.03), Vector3(0.55, floor_y + 1.72, -4.82), Color(0.96, 0.97, 0.98), 0.16)
	_add_box(root, Vector3(3.00, 0.04, 0.07), Vector3(0.55, floor_y + 1.10, -4.80), Color(0.72, 0.74, 0.76), 0.35)
	_add_cyl(root, 0.16, 0.03, Vector3(0.55, floor_y + 2.55, -4.82), Color(0.95, 0.95, 0.94), 0.3).rotation.x = PI * 0.5
	_add_box(root, Vector3(0.68, 0.94, 0.025), Vector3(2.55, floor_y + 1.68, -4.82), Color(0.76, 0.78, 0.74), 0.6)

	# Lecturer Table on Front Stage
	_add_box(root, Vector3(1.55, 0.04, 0.78), Vector3(0.55, floor_y + 0.84, -3.75), mahogany, 0.38)
	for lx in [-0.15, 1.25]:
		for lz in [-4.08, -3.42]:
			_add_box(root, Vector3(0.05, 0.74, 0.05), Vector3(lx, floor_y + 0.45, lz), Color(0.12, 0.12, 0.14), 0.4)

	# 3. Left Window Wall (x = -3.8) with 4:00 PM Golden Afternoon Sunbeams & Louver Blinds
	var left_wall := _add_box(root, Vector3(0.12, 3.2, 11.5), Vector3(-3.95, floor_y + 1.60, -1.2), wall_cream, 0.85)
	left_wall.cast_shadow = GeometryInstance3D.SHADOW_CASTING_SETTING_OFF
	_add_box(root, Vector3(0.62, 0.82, 9.6), Vector3(-3.55, floor_y + 0.41, -1.2), Color(0.66, 0.44, 0.24), 0.55)
	_add_box(root, Vector3(0.66, 0.04, 9.6), Vector3(-3.53, floor_y + 0.84, -1.2), Color(0.32, 0.35, 0.38), 0.4)

	# Left-Side Sunset Windows (Peach-Gold Horizon -> Lavender Zenith Gradient + Sills + Curtains)
	var grad := Gradient.new()
	grad.colors = PackedColorArray([Color(0.56, 0.48, 0.72), Color(0.96, 0.66, 0.52), Color(1.0, 0.86, 0.58)])
	grad.offsets = PackedFloat32Array([0.0, 0.52, 1.0])
	var grad_tex := GradientTexture2D.new()
	grad_tex.gradient = grad
	grad_tex.fill_from = Vector2(0.5, 0.0)
	grad_tex.fill_to = Vector2(0.5, 1.0)

	var win_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	win_mat.albedo_texture = grad_tex
	win_mat.emission_enabled = true
	win_mat.emission_texture = grad_tex
	win_mat.emission_energy_multiplier = 1.65

	for wz in [-3.4, -1.6, 0.2]:
		var pane := _add_box(root, Vector3(0.012, 1.56, 1.44), Vector3(-3.88, floor_y + 1.86, wz), Color(1, 1, 1), 0.25)
		pane.material_override = win_mat
		pane.cast_shadow = GeometryInstance3D.SHADOW_CASTING_SETTING_OFF
		# Window sill ledge
		_add_box(root, Vector3(0.18, 0.04, 1.56), Vector3(-3.80, floor_y + 1.07, wz), Color(0.78, 0.72, 0.68), 0.5)
		# Crisp window mullions casting clean geometric sunlight patches onto desks & whiteboard
		for h_bar in [1.10, 1.62, 2.12, 2.62]:
			var bar := _add_box(root, Vector3(0.06, 0.045, 1.50), Vector3(-3.82, floor_y + h_bar, wz), Color(0.42, 0.36, 0.34), 0.6)
			bar.cast_shadow = GeometryInstance3D.SHADOW_CASTING_SETTING_ON
		for v_off in [-0.72, 0.0, 0.72]:
			var vbar := _add_box(root, Vector3(0.06, 1.56, 0.045), Vector3(-3.82, floor_y + 1.86, wz + v_off), Color(0.42, 0.36, 0.34), 0.6)
			vbar.cast_shadow = GeometryInstance3D.SHADOW_CASTING_SETTING_ON
		# Warm cream curtains framing each window bay
		for c_off in [-0.66, 0.66]:
			_add_box(root, Vector3(0.05, 1.64, 0.16), Vector3(-3.76, floor_y + 1.86, wz + c_off), Color(0.86, 0.74, 0.58), 0.75)

	# Diagonal Golden-Peach Sunset Beams slanting from Left Windows toward Front Whiteboard & Desks
	var beam_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	beam_mat.transparency = BaseMaterial3D.TRANSPARENCY_ALPHA
	beam_mat.blend_mode = BaseMaterial3D.BLEND_MODE_ADD
	beam_mat.shading_mode = BaseMaterial3D.SHADING_MODE_UNSHADED
	beam_mat.cull_mode = BaseMaterial3D.CULL_DISABLED
	beam_mat.depth_draw_mode = BaseMaterial3D.DEPTH_DRAW_DISABLED
	beam_mat.albedo_color = Color(1.0, 0.74, 0.44, 0.032)
	for bz in [-3.4, -1.6, 0.2]:
		var beam := MeshInstance3D.new()
		var bm := BoxMesh.new()
		bm.size = Vector3(5.6, 1.25, 1.28)
		beam.mesh = bm
		beam.position = Vector3(-1.15, floor_y + 1.42, bz - 0.35)
		beam.rotation = Vector3(0.0, -0.18, -0.34)
		beam.material_override = beam_mat
		root.add_child(beam)

	# 4. Right Aisle Wall (x = +2.85) with Tall Cream-Beige Sliding Glass Instrument Cabinets
	_add_box(root, Vector3(0.12, 3.2, 11.5), Vector3(3.05, floor_y + 1.60, -1.2), wall_cream, 0.85)
	for cz in [-3.4, -1.9, -0.4, 1.1]:
		_add_box(root, Vector3(0.56, 2.15, 1.38), Vector3(2.68, floor_y + 1.075, cz), Color(0.84, 0.81, 0.73), 0.55)
		for sy in [0.45, 1.05, 1.60]:
			_add_box(root, Vector3(0.50, 0.025, 1.32), Vector3(2.64, floor_y + sy, cz), Color(0.72, 0.70, 0.64), 0.5)
			_add_box(root, Vector3(0.32, 0.22, 0.45), Vector3(2.62, floor_y + sy + 0.12, cz - 0.30), Color(0.22, 0.35, 0.52), 0.4)
			_add_box(root, Vector3(0.32, 0.22, 0.45), Vector3(2.62, floor_y + sy + 0.12, cz + 0.30), Color(0.76, 0.75, 0.70), 0.4)

	# 5. Neighboring Student Mahogany Lab Tables (2 Rows in Front of You + Left Neighboring Table)
	var neighbor_coords := [
		Vector3(-2.10, 0.0, 0.14),   # Left-side neighboring table next to you
		Vector3(0.02, 0.0, -1.55),   # Row directly in front of you
		Vector3(-2.10, 0.0, -1.55),  # Front-left table
		Vector3(0.02, 0.0, -3.05),   # Second row in front of you
		Vector3(-2.10, 0.0, -3.05)   # Second row left
	]
	for nc in neighbor_coords:
		_add_box(root, Vector3(1.55, 0.04, 0.95), Vector3(nc.x, -0.02, nc.z), mahogany, 0.36)
		for lx in [-0.70, 0.70]:
			for lz in [-0.40, 0.40]:
				_add_box(root, Vector3(0.045, 0.72, 0.045), Vector3(nc.x + lx, -0.38, nc.z + lz), Color(0.14, 0.11, 0.09), 0.45)
		# Student benchtop instruments (oscilloscopes & power supplies)
		_add_box(root, Vector3(0.30, 0.17, 0.24), Vector3(nc.x - 0.28, 0.085, nc.z - 0.10), Color(0.80, 0.79, 0.75), 0.45)
		_add_box(root, Vector3(0.22, 0.14, 0.20), Vector3(nc.x + 0.25, 0.070, nc.z - 0.10), Color(0.25, 0.27, 0.30), 0.45)

	# 6. Detailed Faceted Low-Poly PENS DTE Students (Hijab, Woman, Man) + Walking Aisle Student
	var seated_students: Array[Dictionary] = []
	var student_spots := [
		{"pos": Vector3(-1.78, floor_y, 0.56), "yaw": PI - 0.35, "variant": "hijab"},
		{"pos": Vector3(-2.38, floor_y, 0.62), "yaw": PI + 0.14, "variant": "man"},
		{"pos": Vector3(-0.28, floor_y, -1.05), "yaw": PI - 0.10, "variant": "woman"},
		{"pos": Vector3(0.32, floor_y, -1.05), "yaw": PI + 0.12, "variant": "hijab"},
		{"pos": Vector3(-1.95, floor_y, -1.05), "yaw": PI - 0.15, "variant": "man"},
		{"pos": Vector3(0.08, floor_y, -2.55), "yaw": PI + 0.06, "variant": "woman"}
	]
	for sp in student_spots:
		var st := CampusStudentBuilder.build_student(root, String(sp["variant"]), true)
		st["root"].position = sp["pos"]
		st["root"].rotation.y = float(sp["yaw"])
		seated_students.append(st)

	# Walking Student in the Right Aisle (showcasing DTE + PENS chest crest & yellow sleeve bands)
	var walker := CampusStudentBuilder.build_student(root, "man", false)
	walker["root"].position = Vector3(1.92, floor_y, -2.4)

	var rear_group := Node3D.new(); rear_group.name = "RearRoomProps"; root.add_child(rear_group)
	# 7. Rear Slotted-Angle Steel Racks behind your seat (z = +2.35)
	_add_box(rear_group, Vector3(2.8, 2.05, 0.55), Vector3(-0.4, floor_y + 1.025, 2.35), Color(0.68, 0.67, 0.64), 0.6)
	_add_box(rear_group, Vector3(0.45, 0.32, 0.36), Vector3(0.45, floor_y + 0.92, 2.15), Color(0.92, 0.93, 0.94), 0.4)

	# 8. Acoustic Tile Ceiling + Twin-Tube Fluorescent Lights
	var ceil_slab := _add_box(root, Vector3(9.0, 0.08, 11.5), Vector3(-0.3, floor_y + 3.22, -1.2), Color(0.86, 0.86, 0.84), 0.85)
	ceil_slab.cast_shadow = GeometryInstance3D.SHADOW_CASTING_SETTING_OFF
	var tube_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	tube_mat.albedo_color = Color(1.0, 1.0, 1.0)
	tube_mat.emission_enabled = true
	tube_mat.emission = Color(0.96, 0.97, 1.0)
	tube_mat.emission_energy_multiplier = 0.0
	for fx in [-1.8, 0.8]:
		for fz in [-3.4, -1.5, 0.4]:
			_add_box(root, Vector3(0.28, 0.04, 1.15), Vector3(fx, floor_y + 3.15, fz), Color(0.75, 0.76, 0.78), 0.4)
			var tube := _add_box(root, Vector3(0.18, 0.02, 1.08), Vector3(fx, floor_y + 3.12, fz), Color(1, 1, 1), 0.2)
			tube.material_override = tube_mat

	# Warm Golden Window Sunlight Accent angled onto your workbench from the left windows
	var desk_sun := SpotLight3D.new()
	desk_sun.position = Vector3(-2.65, 1.15, 0.35)
	desk_sun.look_at_from_position(Vector3(-2.65, 1.15, 0.35), Vector3(0.10, 0.05, 0.18), Vector3.UP)
	desk_sun.light_color = Color(1.0, 0.72, 0.42)
	desk_sun.light_energy = 2.6
	desk_sun.spot_range = 5.5
	desk_sun.spot_angle = 42.0
	desk_sun.shadow_enabled = false
	root.add_child(desk_sun)

	# 9. Cinematic Floating Dust Motes in 4:00 PM Sunbeams
	var dust := GPUParticles3D.new()
	dust.name = "AfternoonDustMotes"
	dust.amount = 140
	dust.lifetime = 8.0
	dust.preprocess = 4.0
	dust.position = Vector3(-0.8, 0.45, -1.0)
	var pmat := ParticleProcessMaterial.new()
	pmat.emission_shape = ParticleProcessMaterial.EMISSION_SHAPE_BOX
	pmat.emission_box_extents = Vector3(2.8, 1.1, 2.6)
	pmat.gravity = Vector3(0.004, -0.003, 0.002)
	pmat.initial_velocity_min = 0.005
	pmat.initial_velocity_max = 0.018
	pmat.scale_min = 0.6
	pmat.scale_max = 1.4
	dust.process_material = pmat

	var mote_mesh := SphereMesh.new()
	mote_mesh.radius = 0.0035
	mote_mesh.height = 0.007
	var mote_mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	mote_mat.shading_mode = BaseMaterial3D.SHADING_MODE_UNSHADED
	mote_mat.transparency = BaseMaterial3D.TRANSPARENCY_ALPHA
	mote_mat.albedo_color = Color(1.0, 0.88, 0.58, 0.85)
	mote_mesh.material = mote_mat
	dust.draw_pass_1 = mote_mesh
	root.add_child(dust)

	return {
		"seated": seated_students,
		"walker": walker,
		"time": 0.0
	}

static func animate_lab_life(env_data: Dictionary, delta: float) -> void:
	if env_data.is_empty():
		return
	var campus_group: Node3D = env_data.get("campus_group")
	if campus_group == null or not campus_group.visible:
		return
	var anim: Dictionary = env_data.get("campus_anim", {})
	if anim.is_empty():
		return
	anim["time"] = float(anim.get("time", 0.0)) + delta
	var t := float(anim["time"])

	# Seated practicum students adjusting oscilloscope knobs & writing lab notes
	var seated: Array = anim.get("seated", [])
	for i in range(seated.size()):
		var st: Dictionary = seated[i]
		var torso: Node3D = st["torso"]
		var head: Node3D = st["head"]
		var arm_r: Dictionary = st["arm_r"]
		var phase := t * 1.45 + float(i) * 1.8
		torso.rotation.x = 0.16 + sin(phase) * 0.035
		head.rotation.y = sin(phase * 0.6) * 0.14
		arm_r["shoulder"].rotation.x = -0.84 + cos(phase * 1.5) * 0.10
		arm_r["elbow"].rotation.x = -0.72 + sin(phase * 1.5) * 0.08

	# Walking student pacing along the right aisle (z = -3.0 to +0.8)
	var walker: Dictionary = anim.get("walker", {})
	if not walker.is_empty():
		var w_root: Node3D = walker["root"]
		var cycle := fmod(t * 0.20, 2.0)
		var forward := cycle < 1.0
		var u := cycle if forward else (2.0 - cycle)
		w_root.position.z = lerpf(-3.0, 0.8, u)
		w_root.rotation.y = 0.0 if forward else PI
		var stride := sin(t * 5.0) * 0.42
		var knee_bend := maxf(0.0, -sin(t * 5.0)) * 0.48
		var knee_bend_r := maxf(0.0, sin(t * 5.0)) * 0.48
		walker["leg_l"]["hip"].rotation.x = stride
		walker["leg_l"]["knee"].rotation.x = knee_bend
		walker["leg_r"]["hip"].rotation.x = -stride
		walker["leg_r"]["knee"].rotation.x = knee_bend_r
		walker["arm_l"]["shoulder"].rotation.x = -stride * 0.65
		walker["arm_r"]["shoulder"].rotation.x = stride * 0.65

static func apply_theme(env_data: Dictionary, world_env: WorldEnvironment, key_light: DirectionalLight3D, theme_idx: int) -> void:
	if env_data.is_empty():
		return
	var cfg: Dictionary = LAB_THEMES[clampi(theme_idx, 0, LAB_THEMES.size() - 1)]
	var desk_mat: StandardMaterial3D = env_data["desk_mat"]
	var esd_mat: StandardMaterial3D = env_data["esd_mat"]
	var wall_mat: StandardMaterial3D = env_data["wall_mat"]
	var bulb_mat: StandardMaterial3D = env_data["bulb_mat"]
	var task_light: OmniLight3D = env_data["task_light"]
	var studio_group: Node3D = env_data.get("studio_group")
	var campus_group: Node3D = env_data.get("campus_group")

	var is_campus := bool(cfg.get("is_campus", false))
	if studio_group != null:
		studio_group.visible = not is_campus
	if campus_group != null:
		campus_group.visible = is_campus

	desk_mat.albedo_color = cfg["desk_col"]
	desk_mat.roughness = float(cfg["desk_rough"])
	esd_mat.albedo_color = cfg["esd_col"]
	wall_mat.albedo_color = cfg["wall_col"]

	var lamp_on := bool(cfg["lamp_on"]) and (not is_campus)
	bulb_mat.emission = cfg["lamp_col"]
	bulb_mat.emission_energy_multiplier = 2.8 if lamp_on else 0.0
	task_light.light_color = cfg["lamp_col"]
	task_light.light_energy = float(cfg["lamp_energy"]) if lamp_on else 0.0

	if world_env != null and world_env.environment != null:
		world_env.environment.background_color = cfg["bg_col"]
		world_env.environment.ambient_light_color = cfg["amb_col"]
		world_env.environment.ambient_light_energy = float(cfg["amb_energy"])
		world_env.environment.tonemap_mode = Environment.TONE_MAPPER_ACES if is_campus else Environment.TONE_MAPPER_LINEAR
		world_env.environment.glow_enabled = is_campus
		world_env.environment.glow_intensity = 0.32
		world_env.environment.glow_bloom = 0.12
	if key_light != null:
		key_light.light_color = cfg["key_col"]
		key_light.light_energy = float(cfg["key_energy"])
		if cfg.has("key_rot"):
			key_light.rotation = cfg["key_rot"]

static func _add_box(parent: Node3D, sz: Vector3, pos: Vector3, col: Color, rough := 0.5) -> MeshInstance3D:
	var mi := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var bm := BoxMesh.new()
	bm.size = sz
	mi.mesh = bm
	mi.position = pos
	var mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	mat.albedo_color = col
	mat.roughness = rough
	mi.material_override = mat
	parent.add_child(mi)
	return mi

static func _add_cyl(parent: Node3D, rad: float, h: float, pos: Vector3, col: Color, rough := 0.5, metal := 0.0) -> MeshInstance3D:
	var mi := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var cm := CylinderMesh.new()
	cm.top_radius = rad
	cm.bottom_radius = rad
	cm.height = h
	mi.mesh = cm
	mi.position = pos
	var mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	mat.albedo_color = col
	mat.roughness = rough
	mat.metallic = metal
	mi.material_override = mat
	parent.add_child(mi)
	return mi

static func _add_strut(parent: Node3D, a: Vector3, b: Vector3, rad: float, col: Color) -> void:
	var dist := a.distance_to(b)
	if dist < 0.001:
		return
	var pivot := Node3D.new()
	pivot.position = (a + b) * 0.5
	parent.add_child(pivot)
	var up := Vector3.UP if absf((b - a).normalized().dot(Vector3.UP)) < 0.99 else Vector3.FORWARD
	pivot.look_at(parent.to_global(b), up)
	var cyl := _add_cyl(pivot, rad, dist, Vector3.ZERO, col, 0.35, 0.72)
	cyl.rotation.x = PI * 0.5

static func _add_joint_sphere(parent: Node3D, pos: Vector3, rad: float, col: Color) -> void:
	var mi := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var sm := SphereMesh.new()
	sm.radius = rad
	sm.height = rad * 2.0
	mi.mesh = sm
	mi.position = pos
	var mat := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	mat.albedo_color = col
	mat.roughness = 0.32
	mat.metallic = 0.78
	mi.material_override = mat
	parent.add_child(mi)

static func update_view_occlusion(env_data: Dictionary, cam: Camera3D) -> void:
	if env_data.is_empty() or cam == null:
		return
	var campus_group: Node3D = env_data.get("campus_group")
	if campus_group == null or not campus_group.visible:
		return
	var cam_fwd := -cam.global_transform.basis.z
	var cam_pos := cam.global_transform.origin
	var rear_group := campus_group.get_node_or_null("RearRoomProps") as Node3D
	if rear_group != null:
		# Only render rear storage racks behind the player if camera turns backward (+Z)
		rear_group.visible = cam_fwd.z > -0.15
	# Cull any student avatar outside the camera's forward cone (dot < -0.15)
	var anim: Dictionary = env_data.get("campus_anim", {})
	for st in anim.get("seated", []):
		var r: Node3D = st["root"]
		var to_obj := (r.global_position + Vector3(0, 0.7, 0) - cam_pos).normalized()
		r.visible = cam_fwd.dot(to_obj) > -0.15
	var walker: Dictionary = anim.get("walker", {})
	if not walker.is_empty():
		var wr: Node3D = walker["root"]
		var to_w := (wr.global_position + Vector3(0, 0.8, 0) - cam_pos).normalized()
		wr.visible = cam_fwd.dot(to_w) > -0.15

========================================
FILE: scripts/visualization/psu_3d_builder.gd
========================================
class_name Psu3DBuilder
extends RefCounted

static func _mat(col: Color, rough: float = 0.45, metal: float = 0.1, emit: Color = Color.BLACK, emit_energy: float = 0.0) -> StandardMaterial3D:
	var m := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	m.albedo_color = col
	m.roughness = rough
	m.metallic = metal
	if emit_energy > 0.0:
		m.emission_enabled = true
		m.emission = emit
		m.emission_energy_multiplier = emit_energy
	return m

static func _box(parent: Node3D, name_str: String, sz: Vector3, pos: Vector3, mat: StandardMaterial3D) -> MeshInstance3D:
	var mi := MeshInstance3D.new()
	mi.name = name_str
	var bm := BoxMesh.new()
	bm.size = sz
	mi.mesh = bm
	mi.position = pos
	mi.material_override = mat
	parent.add_child(mi)
	return mi

static func _cyl(parent: Node3D, name_str: String, r: float, h: float, pos: Vector3, rot: Vector3, mat: StandardMaterial3D) -> MeshInstance3D:
	var mi := MeshInstance3D.new()
	mi.name = name_str
	var cm := CylinderMesh.new()
	cm.top_radius = r
	cm.bottom_radius = r
	cm.height = h
	cm.radial_segments = 18
	mi.mesh = cm
	mi.position = pos
	mi.rotation = rot
	mi.material_override = mat
	parent.add_child(mi)
	return mi

static func _lbl(parent: Node3D, text_str: String, pos: Vector3, px: int, col: Color, rot_y: float = 0.0) -> Label3D:
	var l := Label3D.new()
	l.text = text_str
	l.pixel_size = 0.00018
	l.font_size = maxi(px * 2, 22)
	l.outline_size = 0
	l.modulate = col
	l.position = pos
	l.rotation = Vector3(0.0, rot_y, 0.0)
	parent.add_child(l)
	return l

static func _add_psu_knob(parent: Node3D, param_id: String, title: String, pos: Vector3, min_v: float, max_v: float, step_v: float, init_v: float, dial_ticks: Array, interactables: Array) -> Node3D:
	var holder := Node3D.new()
	holder.name = "PsuHolder_" + param_id
	holder.position = pos
	parent.add_child(holder)

	var tick_col := Color(0.16, 0.18, 0.21)
	for ti in range(dial_ticks.size()):
		var frac := float(ti) / float(maxi(dial_ticks.size() - 1, 1))
		var ang := lerpf(deg_to_rad(135.0), deg_to_rad(-135.0), frac)
		var dir := Vector2(-sin(ang), cos(ang))
		var t_mi := _box(holder, "Tick_" + str(ti), Vector3(0.0006, 0.0018, 0.0008), Vector3(dir.x * 0.0102, dir.y * 0.0102, 0.0014), _mat(tick_col, 0.5))
		t_mi.rotation.z = ang
		var l_nd := _lbl(holder, str(dial_ticks[ti]), Vector3(dir.x * 0.0136, dir.y * 0.0136, 0.0016), 10, tick_col)
		l_nd.pixel_size = 0.00017

	var pivot := Node3D.new()
	pivot.name = "PsuKnob_" + param_id
	holder.add_child(pivot)
	var init_norm := clampf((init_v - min_v) / maxf(max_v - min_v, 1e-6), 0.0, 1.0)
	pivot.rotation.z = lerpf(deg_to_rad(135.0), deg_to_rad(-135.0), init_norm)
	_cyl(pivot, "Skirt", 0.0085, 0.003, Vector3(0, 0, 0.0015), Vector3(PI * 0.5, 0, 0), _mat(Color(0.28, 0.30, 0.33), 0.45, 0.2))
	_cyl(pivot, "Body", 0.0072, 0.011, Vector3(0, 0, 0.0065), Vector3(PI * 0.5, 0, 0), _mat(Color(0.76, 0.78, 0.81), 0.35, 0.1))
	_cyl(pivot, "Cap", 0.0056, 0.002, Vector3(0, 0, 0.0122), Vector3(PI * 0.5, 0, 0), _mat(Color(0.32, 0.34, 0.38), 0.35, 0.2))
	_box(pivot, "Pointer", Vector3(0.0014, 0.0055, 0.0025), Vector3(0.0, 0.0030, 0.0128), _mat(Color(0.96, 0.96, 0.98), 0.25))
	interactables.append({
		"type": "knob",
		"param_id": param_id,
		"title": title,
		"pivot": pivot,
		"rotor": pivot,
		"focus_node": pivot,
		"hit_radius": 0.014,
		"ring_radius": 0.011,
		"min": min_v,
		"max": max_v,
		"step": step_v,
		"value": init_v,
		"labels": []
	})
	return pivot

static func _wire_segment(parent: Node, p0: Vector3, p1: Vector3, radius: float, mat: StandardMaterial3D) -> void:
	var diff := p1 - p0
	var length := diff.length()
	if length < 0.0005:
		return
	var mi := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var cm := CylinderMesh.new()
	cm.top_radius = radius
	cm.bottom_radius = radius
	cm.height = length
	cm.radial_segments = 12
	mi.mesh = cm
	mi.material_override = mat
	mi.position = (p0 + p1) * 0.5
	parent.add_child(mi)
	var up := diff.normalized()
	if absf(up.dot(Vector3.UP)) > 0.999:
		mi.rotation = Vector3.ZERO
	else:
		var axis := Vector3.UP.cross(up).normalized()
		var angle := acos(clampf(Vector3.UP.dot(up), -1.0, 1.0))
		mi.basis = Basis(axis, angle)

static func build_gps3030d_on_bench(parent: Node, board_root: Node3D, interactables: Array = []) -> Dictionary:
	var root := Node3D.new()
	root.name = "GWInstek_GPS3030D"
	root.position = Vector3(0.31, 0.003, 0.11)
	root.rotation = Vector3(0.0, -0.46, 0.0)
	parent.add_child(root)

	var chassis_mat := _mat(Color(0.83, 0.84, 0.86, 1.0), 0.42, 0.15)
	var panel_mat := _mat(Color(0.77, 0.78, 0.80, 1.0), 0.45, 0.12)
	var bezel_mat := _mat(Color(0.24, 0.26, 0.29, 1.0), 0.50, 0.10)
	var screen_mat := _mat(Color(0.05, 0.03, 0.03, 1.0), 0.20, 0.05)
	var dark_mat := _mat(Color(0.12, 0.13, 0.14, 1.0), 0.55, 0.10)
	var metal_mat := _mat(Color(0.78, 0.80, 0.83, 1.0), 0.25, 0.85)
	var red_led := _mat(Color(1.0, 0.18, 0.14, 1.0), 0.25, 0.0, Color(1.0, 0.16, 0.12, 1.0), 1.8)
	var green_led := _mat(Color(0.15, 0.92, 0.36, 1.0), 0.25, 0.0, Color(0.12, 0.88, 0.30, 1.0), 1.6)
	var pwr_green := _mat(Color(0.08, 0.46, 0.22, 1.0), 0.38, 0.05)

	var W := 0.124
	var H := 0.140
	var D := 0.215
	var fz := D * 0.5
	_box(root, "Enclosure", Vector3(W, H, D), Vector3(0.0, H * 0.5 + 0.005, 0.0), chassis_mat)
	_box(root, "FrontFace", Vector3(W - 0.006, H - 0.006, 0.003), Vector3(0.0, H * 0.5 + 0.005, fz + 0.001), panel_mat)

	# Side cooling louver slots
	for side_s in [-1.0, 1.0]:
		for vz in [-0.045, -0.015, 0.015, 0.045]:
			_box(root, "Vent", Vector3(0.002, 0.052, 0.014), Vector3(side_s * (W * 0.5 + 0.0005), 0.068, vz), _mat(Color(0.32, 0.34, 0.37), 0.6))

	# Rubber feet & Top Carrying Handle
	for fx in [-0.046, 0.046]:
		for fzz in [-0.084, 0.084]:
			_cyl(root, "Foot", 0.007, 0.005, Vector3(fx, 0.0025, fzz), Vector3.ZERO, dark_mat)
	_box(root, "HandleMountF", Vector3(0.022, 0.008, 0.018), Vector3(0.0, H + 0.009, 0.054), metal_mat)
	_box(root, "HandleMountR", Vector3(0.022, 0.008, 0.018), Vector3(0.0, H + 0.009, -0.054), metal_mat)
	_box(root, "HandleStrap", Vector3(0.018, 0.005, 0.108), Vector3(0.0, H + 0.014, 0.0), dark_mat)

	# Brand & Model Header
	_lbl(root, "GW INSTEK  GPS-3030D", Vector3(-0.014, H - 0.007, fz + 0.003), 16, Color(0.12, 0.14, 0.16))
	_lbl(root, "0-30V / 0-3A", Vector3(0.039, H - 0.007, fz + 0.003), 10, Color(0.22, 0.24, 0.27))

	# Display Bezel + Red 4-Digit 7-Segment LED Readout
	_box(root, "DisplayBezel", Vector3(0.102, 0.036, 0.004), Vector3(0.0, H - 0.031, fz + 0.003), bezel_mat)
	_box(root, "DisplayWindow", Vector3(0.090, 0.026, 0.002), Vector3(0.0, H - 0.031, fz + 0.0048), screen_mat)
	var readout_lbl := _lbl(root, "12.00 V", Vector3(-0.009, H - 0.031, fz + 0.0064), 32, Color(1.0, 0.20, 0.15))
	var volts_led := _cyl(root, "VoltsLed", 0.0018, 0.002, Vector3(0.027, H - 0.025, fz + 0.0058), Vector3(PI * 0.5, 0, 0), red_led)
	var amps_led := _cyl(root, "AmpsLed", 0.0018, 0.002, Vector3(0.027, H - 0.037, fz + 0.0058), Vector3(PI * 0.5, 0, 0), red_led)
	amps_led.visible = false
	_lbl(root, "VOLTS", Vector3(0.036, H - 0.025, fz + 0.0060), 9, Color(0.94, 0.94, 0.94))
	_lbl(root, "AMPS", Vector3(0.036, H - 0.037, fz + 0.0060), 9, Color(0.82, 0.82, 0.82))

	# CURRENT & VOLTAGE Fine/Coarse Interactive Knobs + Silkscreen
	_lbl(root, "CURRENT", Vector3(-0.033, 0.082, fz + 0.003), 11, Color(0.14, 0.16, 0.18))
	_lbl(root, "VOLTAGE", Vector3(0.033, 0.082, fz + 0.003), 11, Color(0.14, 0.16, 0.18))
	_add_psu_knob(root, "psu_current_limit", "PSU CURRENT LIMIT (A)", Vector3(-0.035, 0.066, fz + 0.003), 0.1, 3.0, 0.1, 1.5, ["0.1", "1.5", "3.0"], interactables)
	_add_psu_knob(root, "psu_voltage_fine", "PSU VOLTAGE FINE (±1V)", Vector3(0.017, 0.066, fz + 0.003), -1.0, 1.0, 0.1, 0.0, ["-1", "0", "+1"], interactables)
	_add_psu_knob(root, "psu_voltage_coarse", "PSU VOLTAGE COARSE (0-30V)", Vector3(0.046, 0.066, fz + 0.003), 0.0, 30.0, 1.0, 12.0, ["0", "15", "30"], interactables)
	_lbl(root, "LIMIT", Vector3(-0.033, 0.050, fz + 0.003), 9, Color(0.22, 0.24, 0.26))
	_lbl(root, "FINE", Vector3(0.019, 0.050, fz + 0.003), 9, Color(0.22, 0.24, 0.26))
	_lbl(root, "COARSE", Vector3(0.045, 0.050, fz + 0.003), 9, Color(0.22, 0.24, 0.26))

	_cyl(root, "CC_Led", 0.0018, 0.002, Vector3(-0.014, 0.065, fz + 0.003), Vector3(PI * 0.5, 0, 0), _mat(Color(0.45, 0.12, 0.12), 0.4))
	var cv_led := _cyl(root, "CV_Led", 0.0020, 0.002, Vector3(0.002, 0.065, fz + 0.003), Vector3(PI * 0.5, 0, 0), green_led)
	_lbl(root, "C.V.", Vector3(0.002, 0.057, fz + 0.003), 9, Color(0.12, 0.45, 0.20))

	# Interactive Green POWER Switch
	var pwr_btn := _box(root, "PowerSwitch", Vector3(0.025, 0.012, 0.008), Vector3(-0.035, 0.025, fz + 0.004), pwr_green)
	_lbl(root, "POWER", Vector3(-0.035, 0.036, fz + 0.003), 10, Color(0.14, 0.16, 0.18))
	interactables.append({
		"type": "button",
		"param_id": "psu_power_on",
		"title": "GW Instek GPS-3030D POWER (ON/OFF)",
		"mesh": pwr_btn,
		"focus_node": pwr_btn,
		"hit_radius": 0.016,
		"ring_radius": 0.013
	})

	# 3 Output Binding Posts (- Black, GND Green, + Red)
	var post_y := 0.024
	var posts := [
		{"name": "PostNeg", "x": 0.002, "lbl": "-", "col": Color(0.12, 0.12, 0.14)},
		{"name": "PostGnd", "x": 0.023, "lbl": "GND", "col": Color(0.12, 0.62, 0.28)},
		{"name": "PostPos", "x": 0.044, "lbl": "+", "col": Color(0.88, 0.15, 0.15)},
	]
	var post_local := {}
	for p in posts:
		var px: float = p["x"]
		_lbl(root, p["lbl"], Vector3(px, post_y + 0.013, fz + 0.003), 11, Color(0.14, 0.16, 0.18))
		_cyl(root, p["name"] + "_Collar", 0.0058, 0.007, Vector3(px, post_y, fz + 0.004), Vector3(PI * 0.5, 0, 0), _mat(p["col"], 0.35, 0.1))
		_cyl(root, p["name"] + "_Tip", 0.0034, 0.011, Vector3(px, post_y, fz + 0.010), Vector3(PI * 0.5, 0, 0), metal_mat)
		post_local[p["lbl"]] = Vector3(px, post_y, fz + 0.014)

	# Route Visible 3D Power (+12V Red) and Ground (GND Black) Leads from PSU Binding Posts to PCB Terminals
	var p_pos_world: Vector3 = root.transform * post_local["+"]
	var p_gnd_world: Vector3 = root.transform * post_local["GND"]
	var b_pos: Vector3 = board_root.position if board_root != null else Vector3(0.0, 0.003, 0.255)
	var pcb_vcc_world := b_pos + Vector3(0.072, 0.014, -0.025)
	var pcb_gnd_world := b_pos + Vector3(0.072, 0.014, 0.025)

	var red_wire_mat := _mat(Color(0.94, 0.14, 0.14, 1.0), 0.35, 0.05)
	var blk_wire_mat := _mat(Color(0.10, 0.11, 0.13, 1.0), 0.40, 0.05)
	var red_boot_mat := _mat(Color(0.85, 0.12, 0.12, 1.0), 0.45)
	var blk_boot_mat := _mat(Color(0.14, 0.15, 0.18, 1.0), 0.45)

	# Banana plug boots at PSU posts & clip boots at PCB terminals
	_wire_segment(parent, p_pos_world, p_pos_world + (root.transform.basis.z * 0.018), 0.0036, red_boot_mat)
	_wire_segment(parent, p_gnd_world, p_gnd_world + (root.transform.basis.z * 0.018), 0.0036, blk_boot_mat)
	_wire_segment(parent, pcb_vcc_world + Vector3(0.016, 0.008, -0.004), pcb_vcc_world, 0.0032, red_boot_mat)
	_wire_segment(parent, pcb_gnd_world + Vector3(0.016, 0.008, 0.006), pcb_gnd_world, 0.0032, blk_boot_mat)

	var vcc_pts := [
		p_pos_world + (root.transform.basis.z * 0.017),
		Vector3(0.24, 0.020, 0.205),
		Vector3(0.16, 0.016, 0.220),
		pcb_vcc_world + Vector3(0.015, 0.008, -0.004)
	]
	var gnd_pts := [
		p_gnd_world + (root.transform.basis.z * 0.017),
		Vector3(0.22, 0.019, 0.245),
		Vector3(0.15, 0.015, 0.275),
		pcb_gnd_world + Vector3(0.015, 0.008, 0.006)
	]
	for i in range(vcc_pts.size() - 1):
		_wire_segment(parent, vcc_pts[i], vcc_pts[i + 1], 0.0023, red_wire_mat)
	for i in range(gnd_pts.size() - 1):
		_wire_segment(parent, gnd_pts[i], gnd_pts[i + 1], 0.0023, blk_wire_mat)

	return {
		"root": root,
		"readout_lbl": readout_lbl,
		"volts_led": volts_led,
		"amps_led": amps_led,
		"cv_led": cv_led,
		"show_amps": false
	}

const COL_CABINET := Color(0.58, 0.56, 0.52, 1.0)

static func build_oscilloscope(parent: Node, crt_texture: ViewportTexture) -> Dictionary:
	var root := Node3D.new()

		for fz in [-0.135, 0.135]:
			_add_cyl(root, "Foot", 0.018, 0.016, Vector3(fx, 0.008, fz), Vector3.ZERO, foot_mat)

	_add_box(root, "HandleRight", Vector3(0.014, 0.026, 0.024), Vector3(0.103, 0.210, 0.02), handle_mat)

	var face_z := 0.181
	_add_box(root, "Faceplate", Vector3(0.372, 0.182, 0.004), Vector3(0.0, 0.105, face_z), _mat(COL_FACEPLATE, 0.45, 0.35))

	var probe1 := _build_p6100_probe(parent, 1, "Probe 1 (Yellow)", Color(0.95, 0.80, 0.15, 1.0), interactables, true)

static func _build_p6100_probe(parent: Node, idx: int, label_prefix: String, ring_color: Color, interactables: Array[Dictionary], has_atten_switch: bool) -> Dictionary:
	var asm := Node3D.new()
	asm.name = "P6100_Probe_" + str(idx)
	parent.add_child(asm)

	var grey_body_mat := _mat(Color(0.32, 0.34, 0.36, 1.0), 0.45, 0.15)
	var dark_boot_mat := _mat(Color(0.14, 0.14, 0.15, 1.0), 0.65, 0.05)
	var id_ring_mat := _mat(ring_color, 0.35, 0.05)
	var metal_mat := _mat(COL_METAL_BNC, 0.22, 0.85)

	# 1. BNC Connector End (Metallic locking collar + grey housing + color ID ring + ribbed strain relief)
	var bnc_plug := Node3D.new()
	bnc_plug.name = "BNCPlug_" + str(idx)
	asm.add_child(bnc_plug)
	_add_cyl(bnc_plug, "MetalCollar", 0.0072, 0.014, Vector3(0, 0, 0.007), Vector3(PI * 0.5, 0, 0), metal_mat)
	_add_cyl(bnc_plug, "GreyHousing", 0.0076, 0.018, Vector3(0, 0, 0.022), Vector3(PI * 0.5, 0, 0), grey_body_mat)
	_add_cyl(bnc_plug, "ColorRingBNC", 0.0079, 0.003, Vector3(0, 0, 0.032), Vector3(PI * 0.5, 0, 0), id_ring_mat)
	_add_cyl(bnc_plug, "StrainBoot", 0.0048, 0.022, Vector3(0, 0, 0.044), Vector3(PI * 0.5, 0, 0), dark_boot_mat)

	var bnc_grab := Node3D.new()
	bnc_grab.position = Vector3(0, 0, 0.022)
	bnc_plug.add_child(bnc_grab)
	interactables.append({
		"type": "draggable_bnc",
		"probe_idx": idx,
		"title": "%s BNC Plug (Drag to Scope CH1 or CH2 INPUT)" % label_prefix,
		"focus_node": bnc_grab,
		"hit_radius": 0.026,
		"ring_radius": 0.016
	})

	# 2. P6100 Hook Probe Barrel (Hook tip + tapered nose + finger guard + color ID ring + red 1x/10x switch + GND alligator clip)
	var probe_tip := Node3D.new()
	probe_tip.name = "ProbeHandle_" + str(idx)
	asm.add_child(probe_tip)

	_add_cyl(probe_tip, "HookPin", 0.0016, 0.016, Vector3(0, 0, 0.004), Vector3(PI * 0.5, 0, 0), metal_mat)
	_add_cyl(probe_tip, "HookSheath", 0.0032, 0.022, Vector3(0, 0, 0.018), Vector3(PI * 0.5, 0, 0), grey_body_mat)
	_add_cyl(probe_tip, "FingerGuard", 0.0085, 0.003, Vector3(0, 0, 0.030), Vector3(PI * 0.5, 0, 0), grey_body_mat)
	_add_cyl(probe_tip, "MainBarrel", 0.0058, 0.052, Vector3(0, 0, 0.056), Vector3(PI * 0.5, 0, 0), grey_body_mat)
	_add_cyl(probe_tip, "ColorRingTip", 0.0061, 0.003, Vector3(0, 0, 0.078), Vector3(PI * 0.5, 0, 0), id_ring_mat)
	_add_cyl(probe_tip, "TailBoot", 0.0040, 0.018, Vector3(0, 0, 0.090), Vector3(PI * 0.5, 0, 0), dark_boot_mat)

	# Ground Alligator Clip Lead branching off collar
	_add_cyl(probe_tip, "GndWire", 0.0012, 0.026, Vector3(0.007, -0.005, 0.072), Vector3(PI * 0.48, 0.14, 0), dark_boot_mat)
	_add_box(probe_tip, "AlligatorClip", Vector3(0.004, 0.004, 0.014), Vector3(0.010, -0.006, 0.086), dark_boot_mat)

	if has_atten_switch:
		var sw_id := "probe_attenuation_switch" if idx == 1 else "probe2_attenuation_switch"
		_add_slide_switch_3d(probe_tip, sw_id, "1x / 10x", Vector3(0.0, 0.0075, 0.062), ["1x", "10x"], 0, false, interactables)

	var tip_grab := Node3D.new()
	tip_grab.position = Vector3(0, 0, 0.022)
	probe_tip.add_child(tip_grab)
	interactables.append({
		"type": "draggable_tip",
		"probe_idx": idx,
		"title": "%s Hook Tip (Drag to FG CH1/CH2 OUT or CAL 1Vp-p)" % label_prefix,
		"focus_node": tip_grab,
		"hit_radius": 0.022,
		"ring_radius": 0.016
	})

	# 28-Segment Solid 3D Cylindrical Coaxial Cable
	var cable_segments: Array[MeshInstance3D] = []
	var cable_mat := _mat(Color(0.11, 0.11, 0.12, 1.0), 0.55, 0.05)
	for s in range(28):
		var seg := MeshInstance3D.new()
		var cm := CylinderMesh.new()
		cm.top_radius = 0.0024
		cm.bottom_radius = 0.0024
		cm.height = 1.0
		cm.radial_segments = 10
		seg.mesh = cm
		seg.material_override = cable_mat
		asm.add_child(seg)
		cable_segments.append(seg)

	return {
		"idx": idx,
		"bnc_plug": bnc_plug,
		"probe_tip": probe_tip,
		"cable_segments": cable_segments
	}

static func update_single_probe(probe_data: Dictionary, snaps: Dictionary, bnc_ch: String, tip_tgt: String, dragging_kind := "", drag_world := Vector3.ZERO) -> void:
	if probe_data.is_empty():
		return
	var idx: int = int(probe_data["idx"])
	var bnc_plug: Node3D = probe_data["bnc_plug"]
	var probe_tip: Node3D = probe_data["probe_tip"]

	# Position BNC Plug
	if dragging_kind == "bnc":
		bnc_plug.global_position = drag_world
		bnc_plug.rotation = Vector3(0.15, 0.0, 0.0)
	elif snaps.has(bnc_ch):
		var info: Dictionary = snaps[bnc_ch]
		var n: Node3D = info["node"]
		bnc_plug.global_position = n.global_position + info["offset"]
		bnc_plug.rotation = Vector3.ZERO
	else:
		# Rest neatly on ESD mat to left (Probe 1) and right (Probe 2) of breadboard
		var rest_x := -0.18 if idx == 1 else 0.16
		bnc_plug.global_position = Vector3(rest_x, 0.011, 0.22)
		bnc_plug.rotation = Vector3(0.0, 0.18 if idx == 1 else -0.18, 0.0)

	# Position Probe Tip
	if dragging_kind == "tip":
		probe_tip.global_position = drag_world
		probe_tip.rotation = Vector3(-0.20, 0.0, 0.0)
	elif snaps.has(tip_tgt):
		var info: Dictionary = snaps[tip_tgt]
		var n: Node3D = info["node"]
		var offset_v: Vector3 = info["offset"]
		# Cleanly seat hook pin onto CAL loop (slight vertical stack if both probes share CAL)
		if idx == 2 and tip_tgt == "CAL_1VPP":
			offset_v += Vector3(0.0, -0.0025, 0.001)
		probe_tip.global_position = n.global_position + offset_v
		probe_tip.rotation = Vector3(-0.08, -0.06 if tip_tgt == "CAL_1VPP" else -0.15, 0.0)
	else:
		# Rest neatly on ESD mat clear of breadboard without crossing cables
		var rest_tx := -0.14 if idx == 1 else 0.13
		probe_tip.global_position = Vector3(rest_tx, 0.011, 0.33)
		probe_tip.rotation = Vector3(0.0, 0.32 if idx == 1 else -0.32, 0.0)

	# Update 28-Segment 3D Coaxial Cable (scale applied AFTER look_at!)
	var p0 := bnc_plug.global_position + bnc_plug.global_transform.basis.z * 0.052
	var p1 := probe_tip.global_position + probe_tip.global_transform.basis.z * 0.096
	var segs: Array = probe_data["cable_segments"]
	var count := segs.size()

	var pts: Array[Vector3] = []
	for i in range(count + 1):
		var t := float(i) / float(count)
		var pt := p0.lerp(p1, t)
		pt.z += sin(t * PI) * (0.065 if idx == 1 else 0.090)
		pt.y = maxf(pt.y - sin(t * PI) * 0.045, 0.004)
		pts.append(pt)

	for i in range(count):
		var a := pts[i]
		var b := pts[i + 1]
		var seg: MeshInstance3D = segs[i]
		var diff := b - a
		var length := maxf(diff.length(), 1e-4)
		var mid := (a + b) * 0.5
		var up_dir := diff / length
		if absf(up_dir.dot(Vector3.UP)) > 0.99:
			seg.look_at_from_position(mid, b, Vector3.FORWARD)
		else:
			seg.look_at_from_position(mid, b, Vector3.UP)
		seg.rotate_object_local(Vector3.RIGHT, PI * 0.5)
		seg.scale = Vector3(1.0, length, 1.0)

static func _get_knob_dial_ticks(param_id: String, min_v: float, max_v: float, labels: Array) -> Array:
	if labels.size() > 0:
		return labels
	match param_id:
		"fg_frequency":
			return ["100", "1k", "2.5k", "4k", "5k"]
		"fg_amplitude":
			return ["0.2", "2", "4", "6", "8"]
		"fg_offset":
			return ["-3", "-1.5", "0", "+1.5", "+3"]
		"trigger_level", "vert_pos_div", "ch2_pos":
			return ["-4", "-2", "0", "+2", "+4"]
		"horiz_pos_div":
			return ["-5", "-2.5", "0", "+2.5", "+5"]
		"probe_compensation":
			return ["0.65", "1.0", "1.35"]
		_:
			return ["0", "5", "10"]

static func _add_rotary_knob_3d(
	parent: Node3D,
	param_id: String,
	title: String,
	pos: Vector3,
	radius: float,
	cap_color: Color,
	min_v: float,
	max_v: float,
	step_v: float,
	init_v: float,
	labels: Array,
	interactables: Array[Dictionary]
) -> Node3D:
	var holder := Node3D.new()
	holder.name = "Knob_" + param_id
	holder.position = pos
	parent.add_child(holder)

	var is_fg := param_id.begins_with("fg_")
	var is_big_dial := param_id in ["time_per_div", "volts_per_div", "ch2_volts"]
	var tick_col := Color(0.92, 0.94, 0.98) if is_fg else COL_SILKSCREEN
	var title_y := radius + (0.0118 if is_big_dial else 0.0096)
	_add_label_3d(holder, title, Vector3(0.0, title_y, 0.0012), 14, tick_col)

	var skirt_mat := _mat(Color(0.86, 0.84, 0.80, 1.0), 0.4, 0.2)
	_add_cyl(holder, "Skirt", radius * 1.12, 0.003, Vector3(0, 0, 0.0015), Vector3(PI * 0.5, 0, 0), skirt_mat)

	# Circular engraved dial ring around TIME/DIV and VOLTS/DIV (matching real National VP-5231A)
	if is_big_dial:
		var ring_mi := MeshInstance3D.new()
		var torus := TorusMesh.new()
		torus.inner_radius = radius + 0.0068
		torus.outer_radius = radius + 0.0076
		torus.rings = 32
		torus.ring_segments = 8
		ring_mi.mesh = torus
		ring_mi.rotation.x = PI * 0.5
		ring_mi.position = Vector3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0008)
		ring_mi.material_override = _mat(COL_SILKSCREEN, 0.6, 0.0)
		holder.add_child(ring_mi)
		if param_id == "time_per_div":
			var u_ms := _add_label_3d(holder, "ms", Vector3(-radius - 0.0078, radius + 0.0055, 0.0012), 11, COL_SILKSCREEN)
			u_ms.pixel_size = 0.00018
			var u_us := _add_label_3d(holder, "µs", Vector3(radius + 0.0078, radius + 0.0055, 0.0012), 11, COL_SILKSCREEN)
			u_us.pixel_size = 0.00018
		else:
			var u_v := _add_label_3d(holder, "V", Vector3(-radius - 0.0075, radius + 0.0052, 0.0012), 11, COL_SILKSCREEN)
			u_v.pixel_size = 0.00018

	# Radial scale numbers & tick marks around the knob dial (135 deg CCW -> -135 deg CW)
	var dial_ticks := _get_knob_dial_ticks(param_id, min_v, max_v, labels)
	var tick_count := dial_ticks.size()
	var tick_r := radius + 0.0024
	var lbl_r := radius + (0.0049 if is_big_dial else 0.0058)
	for ti in range(tick_count):
		var frac := float(ti) / float(maxi(tick_count - 1, 1))
		var ang := lerpf(deg_to_rad(135.0), deg_to_rad(-135.0), frac)
		var dir := Vector2(-sin(ang), cos(ang))
		var t_box := _add_box(holder, "Tick_" + str(ti), Vector3(0.0006, 0.0016, 0.0008), Vector3(dir.x * tick_r, dir.y * tick_r, 0.0012), _mat(tick_col, 0.5, 0.1))
		t_box.rotation.z = ang
		var l_node := _add_label_3d(holder, str(dial_ticks[ti]), Vector3(dir.x * lbl_r, dir.y * lbl_r, 0.0015), 10, tick_col)
		l_node.pixel_size = 0.00016

	var rotor := Node3D.new()
	rotor.name = "Rotor"
	rotor.position = Vector3(0, 0, 0.003)
	holder.add_child(rotor)

	_add_cyl(rotor, "Body", radius, 0.012, Vector3(0, 0, 0.006), Vector3(PI * 0.5, 0, 0), _mat(COL_KNOB_DARK, 0.55, 0.1))
	_add_cyl(rotor, "Cap", radius * 0.62, 0.002, Vector3(0, 0, 0.0125), Vector3(PI * 0.5, 0, 0), _mat(cap_color, 0.45, 0.15))
	_add_box(rotor, "Pointer", Vector3(0.0018, radius * 0.88, 0.0020), Vector3(0.0, radius * 0.44, 0.0132), _mat(Color(0.96, 0.95, 0.92, 1.0), 0.3, 0.0))

	var norm := clampf((init_v - min_v) / maxf(max_v - min_v, 1e-6), 0.0, 1.0)
	rotor.rotation.z = lerpf(deg_to_rad(135.0), deg_to_rad(-135.0), norm)

	interactables.append({
		"type": "knob",
		"param_id": param_id,
		"title": title,
		"rotor": rotor,
		"focus_node": rotor,
		"hit_radius": maxf(radius * 1.65, 0.014),
		"ring_radius": radius * 1.35,
		"min": min_v,
		"max": max_v,
		"step": step_v,
		"value": init_v,
		"labels": labels
	})
	return rotor

static func _add_slide_switch_3d(
	parent: Node3D,
	param_id: String,
	title: String,
	pos: Vector3,
	options: Array,
	init_idx: int,
	vertical: bool,
	interactables: Array[Dictionary]
) -> Node3D:
	var holder := Node3D.new()
	holder.name = "Switch_" + param_id
	holder.position = pos
	parent.add_child(holder)

	_add_label_3d(holder, title, Vector3(0.0, 0.0135 if vertical else 0.0095, 0.001), 14, COL_SILKSCREEN)

	var slot_size := Vector3(0.006, 0.018, 0.002) if vertical else Vector3(0.022, 0.006, 0.002)
	_add_box(holder, "Slot", slot_size, Vector3(0, 0, 0.001), _mat(Color(0.14, 0.14, 0.14, 1.0), 0.8, 0.1))

	var nub := _add_box(holder, "Actuator", Vector3(0.0055, 0.0055, 0.0075), Vector3(0, 0, 0.004), _mat(Color(0.85, 0.22, 0.18, 1.0) if param_id == "probe_attenuation_switch" else Color(0.28, 0.27, 0.26, 1.0), 0.4, 0.3))

	var meta := {
		"type": "switch",
		"param_id": param_id,
		"title": title,
		"actuator": nub,
		"focus_node": nub,
		"hit_radius": 0.014,
		"ring_radius": 0.012,
		"options": options,
		"index": init_idx,
		"vertical": vertical
	}
	update_switch_visual(meta)
	interactables.append(meta)
	return nub

static func update_switch_visual(meta: Dictionary) -> void:
	var nub: Node3D = meta["actuator"]
	var count: int = maxi(meta["options"].size(), 2)
	var idx: int = clampi(int(meta["index"]), 0, count - 1)
	var frac := float(idx) / float(count - 1)
	var offset := lerpf(-0.006, 0.006, frac)
	if meta["vertical"]:
		nub.position.y = -offset
	else:
		nub.position.x = offset

static func _add_push_button(parent: Node3D, param_id: String, title: String, pos: Vector3, interactables: Array[Dictionary]) -> MeshInstance3D:
	var holder := Node3D.new()
	holder.position = pos
	parent.add_child(holder)

	var cap := _add_box(holder, "ButtonCap", Vector3(0.010, 0.010, 0.008), Vector3(0, 0, 0.003), _mat(Color(0.18, 0.17, 0.17, 1.0), 0.45, 0.2))

	interactables.append({
		"type": "button",
		"param_id": param_id,
		"title": title,
		"cap": cap,
		"focus_node": cap,
		"hit_radius": 0.013,
		"ring_radius": 0.010
	})
	return cap

static func _add_bnc_jack(parent: Node3D, jack_name: String, pos: Vector3, scale_f := 1.0) -> Node3D:
	var holder := Node3D.new()
	holder.name = jack_name
	holder.position = pos
	parent.add_child(holder)

	var metal_mat := _mat(COL_METAL_BNC, 0.25, 0.85)
	var insulator_mat := _mat(Color(0.92, 0.92, 0.90, 1.0), 0.35, 0.0)
	_add_cyl(holder, "Flange", 0.0075 * scale_f, 0.003 * scale_f, Vector3(0, 0, 0.0015), Vector3(PI * 0.5, 0, 0), metal_mat)
	_add_cyl(holder, "Barrel", 0.0052 * scale_f, 0.012 * scale_f, Vector3(0, 0, 0.007 * scale_f), Vector3(PI * 0.5, 0, 0), metal_mat)
	_add_cyl(holder, "Dielectric", 0.0032 * scale_f, 0.0125 * scale_f, Vector3(0, 0, 0.0072 * scale_f), Vector3(PI * 0.5, 0, 0), insulator_mat)
	return holder

static func _add_box(parent: Node3D, node_name: String, sz: Vector3, pos: Vector3, mat: Material) -> MeshInstance3D:
	var mi := MeshInstance3D.new()
	mi.name = node_name
	var bm := BoxMesh.new()
	bm.size = sz
	mi.mesh = bm
	mi.material_override = mat
	mi.position = pos
	parent.add_child(mi)
	return mi

static func _add_cyl(parent: Node3D, node_name: String, r: float, h: float, pos: Vector3, rot: Vector3, mat: Material) -> MeshInstance3D:
	var mi := MeshInstance3D.new()
	mi.name = node_name
	var cm := CylinderMesh.new()
	cm.top_radius = r
	cm.bottom_radius = r
	cm.height = h
	cm.radial_segments = 24
	mi.mesh = cm
	mi.material_override = mat
	mi.position = pos
	mi.rotation = rot
	parent.add_child(mi)
	return mi

static func _add_label_3d(parent: Node3D, text: String, pos: Vector3, px_size: int, col: Color) -> Label3D:
	var l := Label3D.new()
	l.text = text
	l.pixel_size = 0.00024
	l.font_size = px_size
	l.outline_size = 0
	l.modulate = col
	l.no_depth_test = false
	l.shaded = false
	l.position = pos
	parent.add_child(l)
	return l

static func _mat(col: Color, rough: float, metal: float) -> StandardMaterial3D:
	var m := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	m.albedo_color = col
	m.roughness = rough
	m.metallic = metal
	return m

========================================
FILE: scripts/visualization/waveform_canvas.gd
========================================
class_name WaveformCanvas
extends Control

const GRID_COLS := 10
const GRID_ROWS := 8

var _persistence_buffer: Array[PackedVector2Array] = []
var _ch2_persistence: Array[PackedVector2Array] = []
const MAX_PERSISTENCE_FRAMES := 8

func _ready() -> void:
	if has_node("/root/SimState"):
		SimState.state_updated.connect(queue_redraw)

func _draw() -> void:
	var w := size.x
	var h := size.y
	if w < 10.0 or h < 10.0:
		return

	var pad_left := 44.0
	var pad_right := 16.0
	var pad_top := 28.0
	var pad_bottom := 26.0

	var plot_rect := Rect2(
		pad_left,
		pad_top,
		maxf(w - pad_left - pad_right, 10.0),
		maxf(h - pad_top - pad_bottom, 10.0)
	)

	draw_rect(Rect2(Vector2.ZERO, size), Color(0.03, 0.05, 0.05, 1.0), true)

	var power_on := true
	var scale_illum := 0.65
	var intensity := 0.85
	var focus := 0.88
	var persistence := 0.30
	var vert_mode := 1

	if has_node("/root/SimState"):
		power_on = bool(SimState.view_config.get("power_on", true))
		scale_illum = float(SimState.analog_config.get("scale_illum", 0.65))
		intensity = float(SimState.analog_config.get("intensity", 0.85))
		focus = float(SimState.analog_config.get("focus", 0.88))
		persistence = float(SimState.analog_config.get("persistence", 0.30))
		vert_mode = int(SimState.analog_config.get("vert_mode", 1))

	var screen_bg := Color(0.02, 0.11, 0.12, 1.0) if power_on else Color(0.03, 0.06, 0.06, 1.0)
	draw_rect(plot_rect, screen_bg, true)

	_draw_graticule(plot_rect, scale_illum if power_on else 0.15)
	_draw_axis_labels(plot_rect)

	if not power_on:
		_persistence_buffer.clear()
		_ch2_persistence.clear()
		_draw_header_hud(plot_rect, false)
		return

	if not has_node("/root/SimState"):
		return

	var times: PackedFloat64Array = SimState.time_buffer
	if times.size() < 2:
		_draw_header_hud(plot_rect, true)
		return

	var t_start := times[0]
	var t_span := maxf(times[times.size() - 1] - t_start, 1e-9)

	# Draw CH 1 if MODE == CH1 (0) or ALT (1)
	if vert_mode == 0 or vert_mode == 1:
		var pts1 := _build_screen_points(plot_rect, times, SimState.voltage_buffer, t_start, t_span, float(SimState.view_config["volts_per_div"]))
		_draw_phosphor_trace(pts1, _persistence_buffer, Color(0.18, 1.0, 0.72), intensity, focus, persistence)
	else:
		_persistence_buffer.clear()

	# Draw CH 2 if MODE == ALT (1) or CH2 (2)
	if vert_mode == 1 or vert_mode == 2:
		var pts2 := _build_screen_points(plot_rect, times, SimState.ch2_voltage_buffer, t_start, t_span, float(SimState.view_config.get("ch2_volts_per_div", 1.0)))
		_draw_phosphor_trace(pts2, _ch2_persistence, Color(0.35, 0.85, 1.0), intensity, focus, persistence)
	else:
		_ch2_persistence.clear()

	_draw_trigger_marker(plot_rect)
	_draw_header_hud(plot_rect, true)

func _build_screen_points(plot_rect: Rect2, times: PackedFloat64Array, volts: PackedFloat64Array, t_start: float, t_span: float, v_per_div: float) -> PackedVector2Array:
	var pts := PackedVector2Array()
	var n := mini(times.size(), volts.size())
	if n < 2:
		return pts
	pts.resize(n)
	var v_half_span := maxf(v_per_div * (float(GRID_ROWS) * 0.5), 1e-6)
	var center_y := plot_rect.position.y + plot_rect.size.y * 0.5
	var half_h := plot_rect.size.y * 0.5

	for i in range(n):
		var nx := clampf((times[i] - t_start) / t_span, 0.0, 1.0)
		var px := plot_rect.position.x + nx * plot_rect.size.x
		var norm_v := float(volts[i]) / v_half_span
		var py := clampf(center_y - norm_v * half_h, plot_rect.position.y, plot_rect.position.y + plot_rect.size.y)
		pts[i] = Vector2(px, py)
	return pts

func _draw_phosphor_trace(pts: PackedVector2Array, persist_hist: Array[PackedVector2Array], base_col: Color, intensity: float, focus: float, persistence: float) -> void:
	if pts.size() < 2:
		return
	var max_hist := int(round(persistence * float(MAX_PERSISTENCE_FRAMES)))
	if max_hist > 0:
		for h_idx in range(persist_hist.size()):
			var age_alpha := (float(h_idx + 1) / float(persist_hist.size() + 1)) * 0.24 * persistence * intensity
			var old_pts := persist_hist[h_idx]
			if old_pts.size() >= 2:
				draw_polyline(old_pts, Color(base_col.r * 0.4, base_col.g * 0.85, base_col.b * 0.75, age_alpha), 2.2, true)
		persist_hist.append(pts)
		while persist_hist.size() > max_hist:
			persist_hist.pop_front()
	else:
		persist_hist.clear()

	var defocus := 1.0 - focus
	var outer_w := 4.0 + defocus * 7.5
	var core_w := 1.4 + defocus * 2.5
	var outer_alpha := clampf(0.14 + intensity * 0.24, 0.05, 0.48)
	var core_alpha := clampf(0.35 + intensity * 0.65, 0.15, 1.0)

	draw_polyline(pts, Color(base_col.r, base_col.g, base_col.b, outer_alpha), outer_w, true)
	draw_polyline(pts, Color(minf(base_col.r + 0.45, 1.0), 1.0, minf(base_col.b + 0.20, 1.0), core_alpha), core_w, true)

func _draw_graticule(rect: Rect2, illum: float) -> void:
	var alpha := clampf(0.10 + illum * 0.36, 0.08, 0.50)
	var minor_col := Color(0.05, 0.18, 0.20, alpha * 0.65)
	var major_col := Color(0.08, 0.30, 0.33, alpha)
	var center_col := Color(0.12, 0.44, 0.48, minf(alpha * 1.35, 0.68))

	var dx := rect.size.x / float(GRID_COLS)
	var dy := rect.size.y / float(GRID_ROWS)

	for c in range(GRID_COLS + 1):
		var x := rect.position.x + float(c) * dx
		var col := center_col if c == GRID_COLS / 2 else major_col
		draw_line(Vector2(x, rect.position.y), Vector2(x, rect.position.y + rect.size.y), col, 1.0)

	for r in range(GRID_ROWS + 1):
		var y := rect.position.y + float(r) * dy
		var col := center_col if r == GRID_ROWS / 2 else major_col
		draw_line(Vector2(rect.position.x, y), Vector2(rect.position.x + rect.size.x, y), col, 1.0)

	var mid_y := rect.position.y + rect.size.y * 0.5
	var mid_x := rect.position.x + rect.size.x * 0.5
	for c in range(GRID_COLS * 5 + 1):
		var tx := rect.position.x + float(c) * (dx / 5.0)
		draw_line(Vector2(tx, mid_y - 3.0), Vector2(tx, mid_y + 3.0), minor_col, 1.0)
	for r in range(GRID_ROWS * 5 + 1):
		var ty := rect.position.y + float(r) * (dy / 5.0)
		draw_line(Vector2(mid_x - 3.0, ty), Vector2(mid_x + 3.0, ty), minor_col, 1.0)

	draw_rect(rect, Color(0.16, 0.36, 0.38, 0.9), false, 2.0)

func _draw_trigger_marker(rect: Rect2) -> void:
	var trig_v := float(SimState.analog_config.get("trigger_level", 0.0))
	var v_per_div := float(SimState.view_config.get("volts_per_div", 1.0))
	var v_half := maxf(v_per_div * (float(GRID_ROWS) * 0.5), 1e-6)
	var center_y := rect.position.y + rect.size.y * 0.5
	var ty := clampf(center_y - (trig_v / v_half) * (rect.size.y * 0.5), rect.position.y, rect.position.y + rect.size.y)

	var col := Color(1.0, 0.78, 0.25, 0.92) if SimState.is_triggered else Color(0.95, 0.35, 0.30, 0.85)
	var rx := rect.position.x + rect.size.x
	draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([
		Vector2(rx + 2.0, ty),
		Vector2(rx + 10.0, ty - 5.0),
		Vector2(rx + 10.0, ty + 5.0)
	]), col)

func _draw_axis_labels(rect: Rect2) -> void:
	var font := ThemeDB.fallback_font
	var v_per_div := 1.0
	var t_per_div := 0.0005
	if has_node("/root/SimState"):
		v_per_div = float(SimState.view_config["volts_per_div"])
		t_per_div = float(SimState.view_config["time_per_div"])

	var dy := rect.size.y / float(GRID_ROWS)
	for r in range(0, GRID_ROWS + 1, 2):
		var div_offset := (GRID_ROWS / 2) - r
		var v_val := float(div_offset) * v_per_div
		var y := rect.position.y + float(r) * dy + 4.0
		draw_string(font, Vector2(4.0, y), "%.1fV" % v_val, HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 10, Color(0.55, 0.78, 0.78, 0.85))

	var dx := rect.size.x / float(GRID_COLS)
	for c in range(0, GRID_COLS + 1, 2):
		var t_ms := float(c) * t_per_div * 1000.0
		var x := rect.position.x + float(c) * dx - 12.0
		draw_string(font, Vector2(maxf(x, 2.0), rect.position.y + rect.size.y + 15.0), "%.2fms" % t_ms, HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 10, Color(0.55, 0.78, 0.78, 0.85))

func _draw_header_hud(rect: Rect2, power_on: bool) -> void:
	var font := ThemeDB.fallback_font
	if not power_on:
		draw_string(font, Vector2(rect.position.x + 8.0, 18.0), "VP-5231A CRT  |  POWER: OFF", HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 12, Color(0.75, 0.35, 0.30, 0.95))
		return

	var mode_names := ["CH1 ONLY", "ALT (CH1 + CH2)", "CH2 ONLY"]
	var v_mode := int(SimState.analog_config.get("vert_mode", 1))
	var v_div := float(SimState.view_config["volts_per_div"])
	var v2_div := float(SimState.view_config.get("ch2_volts_per_div", 1.0))
	var t_ms := float(SimState.view_config["time_per_div"]) * 1000.0
	var trig_txt := "TRIG'D" if SimState.is_triggered else "AUTO"
	var hud := "%s  |  CH1: %.2fV/d  CH2: %.2fV/d  |  %.2fms/d  |  [%s]" % [
		mode_names[clampi(v_mode, 0, 2)], v_div, v2_div, t_ms, trig_txt
	]
	draw_string(font, Vector2(rect.position.x + 6.0, 18.0), hud, HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 11, Color(0.45, 0.96, 0.78, 0.95))

========================================
FILE: scripts/visualization/workbench_3d.gd

class_name Workbench3D
extends SubViewportContainer

const VOLTS_STEPS := [0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0]
const TIME_STEPS := [0.0001, 0.0002, 0.0005, 0.001, 0.002, 0.005]

const CAMERA_PRESETS := [
	{"name": "1: Full Bench", "pos": Vector3(0.0, 0.28, 0.66), "target": Vector3(0.0, 0.08, 0.12)},
	{"name": "2: Scope Knobs", "pos": Vector3(0.09, 0.14, 0.35), "target": Vector3(0.09, 0.10, 0.14)},
	{"name": "3: CRT + CAL Hook", "pos": Vector3(-0.09, 0.13, 0.33), "target": Vector3(-0.09, 0.10, 0.14)},
	{"name": "4: Siglent Gen", "pos": Vector3(-0.22, 0.14, 0.34), "target": Vector3(-0.34, 0.06, 0.08)},
	{"name": "5: Breadboard + PSU", "pos": Vector3(0.08, 0.16, 0.44), "target": Vector3(0.06, 0.03, 0.22)}
]

var _active_knob := {}
var _dragging_probe_kind := "" # "bnc" or "tip"
var _dragging_probe_idx := 1
var _drag_plane := Plane(Vector3.BACK, 0.20)
var _drag_grab_offset := Vector3.ZERO
var _drag_current_world := Vector3.ZERO
var _drag_target_world := Vector3.ZERO
var _drag_start_y := 0.0
var _drag_start_val := 0.0

# Smooth damped camera state
var _orbiting := false
var _panning := false
var _yaw := 0.0
var _pitch := -0.22
var _dist := 0.50
var _cam_target := Vector3(0.02, 0.09, 0.14)

var _goal_yaw := 0.0
var _goal_pitch := -0.22
var _goal_dist := 0.50
var _goal_target := Vector3(0.02, 0.09, 0.14)

var _pulse_time := 0.0
var _hud_label: Label
